> DEVELOPER

# BOS-ARSA Log — BOS-ARSA Krisenkommunikationsübung Tool

## Claude Code Implementation Prompt

---

### Project Name & Naming

**App name:** BOS-ARSA Log
**Subdomain:** bosarsalog.oeradio.at
**Repo:** `bosarsalog`
**Tagline:** Krisenkommunikationsübung — Live-Erfassung, Multi-User, Auswertung

This tool is part of the oeradio.at ecosystem (BandBlick, StrahlBlick, AkkuBlick, RelaisBlick, QBlitz, FunkPilot, etc.).

---

### What You're Building

A real-time, multi-user web application for the BOS-ARSA weekly ham radio crisis communication exercises. Every Sunday, a team of admin operators queries multiple repeaters and direct frequencies across Austria to collect signal reports from ~100 amateur radio operators. Currently this runs on a shared Excel workbook (~15 MB, 246 columns, 1,066 rows) plus two standalone HTML prototypes — all disconnected. BOS-ARSA Log replaces all three with a single unified tool.

---

### Tech Stack

- **Frontend:** React + TypeScript + Vite + Tailwind CSS
- **Backend:** Node.js + Express + TypeScript
- **Database:** SQLite (via better-sqlite3) — the data volume is small (~1,000 operators, ~200 reports/week), SQLite is perfect and keeps deployment simple
- **Real-time:** Socket.IO for live sync between concurrent admins
- **Auth:** Simple token-based auth with callsign + PIN (no heavy auth framework needed — this is a small admin team of ~5 people)
- **Deployment:** Docker + Docker Compose, Cloudflare Tunnel to bosarsalog.oeradio.at
- **Language:** German UI throughout. Code comments and variable names in English.

---

### CLAUDE.md

Create this at the repo root:

```markdown
# BOS-ARSA Log — BOS-ARSA Krisenkommunikationsübung

## What is this?
A real-time multi-user web app for weekly ham radio crisis communication exercises.
Admins log signal reports from amateur radio operators across Austria on multiple
repeaters and frequencies. Replaces a shared Excel workbook + two HTML prototypes.

## Tech Stack
- Frontend: React 18, TypeScript, Vite, Tailwind CSS, Socket.IO client
- Backend: Node.js, Express, TypeScript, Socket.IO server
- Database: SQLite via better-sqlite3
- Auth: Simple callsign + PIN token auth
- Deployment: Docker, Cloudflare Tunnel

## Architecture
- Monorepo: /client (React SPA) + /server (Express API + WebSocket)
- RESTful API for CRUD, WebSocket for real-time sync
- SQLite DB file at /data/bosarsalog.db (Docker volume mounted)
- Server serves the built client in production

## Key Domain Concepts
- Operator (Rufzeichen): ham radio callsign, e.g. OE8YML, S55UJE
- Repeater/Frequency (Umsetzer): radio relay station with TX freq, offset, CTCSS
- Linked Repeater: one logical repeater spanning multiple physical sites (Einstiegspunkte)
- Einstiegspunkt: physical entry point in a linked repeater network
- Signal Report (Rapport): RST format e.g. "5/9+30 HK" = R5/S9+30dB via Hermannskogel
- Exercise (Übung): one Sunday session with configured repeaters and collected reports
- Bezirk: Austrian political district, identified by 2-3 letter code
- Bundesland: Austrian state (OE1=Wien through OE9=Vorarlberg)

## Conventions
- German UI text, English code
- Callsigns always uppercase
- RST format: R/S with optional +dB modifier and location suffix
- All timestamps in Europe/Vienna timezone
- API prefix: /api/v1/

## Common Commands
- `npm run dev` — start both client and server in dev mode
- `npm run build` — build for production
- `npm run db:seed` — seed database with reference data
- `docker compose up -d` — production deployment
```

---

### Database Schema

Design the SQLite schema with these tables:

#### `operators` — Master registry (~1,066 records)
```
id                INTEGER PRIMARY KEY
callsign          TEXT UNIQUE NOT NULL      -- e.g. "OE8YML"
name              TEXT                      -- first name or nickname
qth               TEXT                      -- location (free text)
bezirk_code       TEXT REFERENCES bezirke(code)
bundesland_code   TEXT REFERENCES bundeslaender(code)
is_bos_arsa       BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE
is_arena          BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE
is_portable       BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE
is_exercise_support BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE
gender            TEXT                      -- 'w' or null
email             TEXT
equipment         TEXT                      -- radio model
antenna           TEXT
has_emergency_power BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE
has_lighthouse_readiness BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE
latitude          REAL
longitude         REAL
created_at        DATETIME DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
updated_at        DATETIME DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
```

#### `bundeslaender` — Reference table (9 Austrian + international)
```
code              TEXT PRIMARY KEY          -- e.g. "01", "08", "10"
name              TEXT NOT NULL             -- e.g. "Kärnten", "SLOWENIEN"
sort_order        INTEGER
is_international  BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE
```

#### `bezirke` — District reference (~103 records)
```
code              TEXT PRIMARY KEY          -- e.g. "KC", "SP", "WC", "HK"
name              TEXT NOT NULL             -- e.g. "Klagenfurt Stadt"
bundesland_code   TEXT REFERENCES bundeslaender(code)
is_capital        BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE
```

#### `repeaters` — Repeater/frequency master list
```
id                INTEGER PRIMARY KEY
short_name        TEXT NOT NULL             -- e.g. "Gerlitze 2m", "Direkte 145.525"
site_name         TEXT                      -- e.g. "Gerlitze"
band              TEXT                      -- "2m", "70cm", "23cm"
callsign          TEXT                      -- e.g. "OE8XNK"
frequency_mhz     REAL                      -- TX frequency
offset_mhz        REAL                      -- e.g. -0.6, -7.6
ctcss_hz          REAL                      -- e.g. 88.5, 123.0
burst_hz          REAL                      -- e.g. 1750
type              TEXT DEFAULT 'repeater'   -- 'repeater' or 'simplex'
is_linked         BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE     -- linked repeater system?
sort_order        INTEGER
```

#### `einstiegspunkte` — Entry points for linked repeaters
```
id                INTEGER PRIMARY KEY
repeater_id       INTEGER REFERENCES repeaters(id)
site_name         TEXT NOT NULL             -- e.g. "Hermannskogel"
callsign          TEXT                      -- e.g. "OE1XAT"
abbreviation      TEXT                      -- e.g. "HK", "SCHÖ", "GB"
bundesland_code   TEXT REFERENCES bundeslaender(code)
sort_order        INTEGER
```

#### `exercises` — Weekly exercise sessions
```
id                INTEGER PRIMARY KEY
date              DATE UNIQUE NOT NULL      -- always a Sunday
status            TEXT DEFAULT 'planned'    -- planned, active, completed
notes             TEXT
created_at        DATETIME DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
```

#### `exercise_repeaters` — Which repeaters are active per exercise
```
exercise_id       INTEGER REFERENCES exercises(id)
repeater_id       INTEGER REFERENCES repeaters(id)
operator_callsign TEXT                      -- OP for this repeater this week
PRIMARY KEY (exercise_id, repeater_id)
```

#### `exercise_attendance` — Who participated
```
exercise_id       INTEGER REFERENCES exercises(id)
operator_id       INTEGER REFERENCES operators(id)
callsign_suffix   TEXT                      -- "/p", "/m", "/8", "remote" etc.
is_present        BOOLEAN DEFAULT TRUE
entered_by        TEXT                      -- admin callsign who logged this
PRIMARY KEY (exercise_id, operator_id)
```

#### `signal_reports` — The core data
```
id                INTEGER PRIMARY KEY
exercise_id       INTEGER REFERENCES exercises(id)
operator_id       INTEGER REFERENCES operators(id)
repeater_id       INTEGER REFERENCES repeaters(id)
readability       INTEGER                   -- 1-5
strength          INTEGER                   -- 1-9
db_over_s9        TEXT                      -- "+10", "+20", "++", "+", null
einstiegspunkt_id INTEGER REFERENCES einstiegspunkte(id)
is_op_marker      BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE     -- true = station operator, not a report
notes             TEXT                      -- "Sonstiges" free text
entered_by        TEXT                      -- admin callsign
created_at        DATETIME DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
updated_at        DATETIME DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
UNIQUE (exercise_id, operator_id, repeater_id)
```

#### `admins` — Admin users
```
id                INTEGER PRIMARY KEY
callsign          TEXT UNIQUE NOT NULL
name              TEXT
pin_hash          TEXT NOT NULL             -- bcrypt hash of PIN
role              TEXT DEFAULT 'data_entry' -- 'admin', 'data_entry', 'viewer'
```

---

### API Endpoints

```
# Auth
POST   /api/v1/auth/login          { callsign, pin } → { token, admin }

# Exercises
GET    /api/v1/exercises            List all (with summary stats)
POST   /api/v1/exercises            Create new exercise { date }
GET    /api/v1/exercises/:id        Full exercise with reports
PATCH  /api/v1/exercises/:id        Update status/notes
GET    /api/v1/exercises/:id/stats  Live statistics

# Exercise Repeaters
GET    /api/v1/exercises/:id/repeaters
POST   /api/v1/exercises/:id/repeaters     Activate repeater for exercise
PATCH  /api/v1/exercises/:id/repeaters/:rid  Update OP assignment

# Signal Reports
GET    /api/v1/exercises/:id/reports
POST   /api/v1/exercises/:id/reports       Create report
PATCH  /api/v1/exercises/:id/reports/:rid  Edit report
DELETE /api/v1/exercises/:id/reports/:rid  Delete report

# Operators
GET    /api/v1/operators            List/search (with ?q= autocomplete)
POST   /api/v1/operators            Quick-add new operator
GET    /api/v1/operators/:id        Full profile with history
PATCH  /api/v1/operators/:id        Update operator

# Reference Data
GET    /api/v1/bundeslaender
GET    /api/v1/bezirke
GET    /api/v1/repeaters
GET    /api/v1/einstiegspunkte

# Reports/Export
GET    /api/v1/exercises/:id/export/bund   TXT export (Bundesland-grouped)
GET    /api/v1/exercises/:id/export/land   TXT export (repeater-grouped)
GET    /api/v1/exercises/:id/export/combined  Combined report
GET    /api/v1/overview                    Gesamtübersicht (annual summary)
GET    /api/v1/exercises/:id/nebenstationen  District breakdown report
```

---

### WebSocket Events (Socket.IO)

```
# Client → Server
join_exercise      { exercise_id }
report_created     { report }
report_updated     { report }
report_deleted     { report_id }
attendance_updated { attendance }

# Server → Client (broadcast to all in exercise room)
report_created     { report, entered_by }
report_updated     { report, entered_by }
report_deleted     { report_id, entered_by }
attendance_updated { attendance, entered_by }
stats_updated      { stats }
```

---

### Frontend Pages & Components

#### 1. Login Page
Simple callsign + PIN form. Warm, professional design.

#### 2. Dashboard / Gesamtübersicht
- Annual calendar showing all Sundays, colored by status (planned/active/completed)
- Summary table: date, participants, total reports per exercise
- Quick action: create or open today's/next exercise

#### 3. Exercise Setup Page (Admin only)
- Select date, activate repeaters from master list
- Assign OP per repeater
- Configure Einstiegspunkte for linked repeaters
- Set exercise to "active"

#### 4. Live Data Entry Page — THE CORE
This is the most critical page. It must support **two modes** simultaneously:

##### Mode A: "Bund" (Geography-first)
- Displayed as Bundesland → Bezirk hierarchy (all 9 + Sonstige)
- Per Bezirk: input row with Rufzeichen, Rapport, Einstiegspunkt dropdown, Sonstiges
- Einstiegspunkt dropdown populated from linked repeater's entry points
- Last selected Einstiegspunkt auto-propagates to all Bezirke in same Bundesland
- Bundesland sections collapsible, with badge showing count per section
- Badge colors: Hauptstadt = red badge (##code), normal = gray badge (#code)

##### Mode B: "Land" (Repeater-first)
- Repeater selector (dropdown or tabs) — only active repeaters for this exercise
- Shows frequency details: frequency, offset, CTCSS/burst
- Below: Bezirk list (configurable — for Kärnten admins: 10 Kärntner Bezirke + Sonstige)
- Per Bezirk: Rufzeichen, Rapport, Sonstiges
- Cross-repeater callsign sync: adding a callsign on one repeater creates empty placeholders on all others

##### Shared Behavior (both modes)
- Auto-complete on Rufzeichen field from operator registry
- Auto-uppercase on Rufzeichen and Rapport fields
- Enter key submits report and clears fields, keeps focus for next entry
- Keyboard shortcuts: Alt+digit → "5/<digit>", Ctrl+digit → "OE<digit>"
- Inline editing of submitted reports (click to edit)
- Delete with confirmation
- Running totals bar: present operators, total reports, per-repeater counts
- Real-time sync: reports entered by other admins appear instantly
- Unsaved data warning before navigation/export
- Quick-add: if callsign not found, show inline "Neu anlegen" form

#### 5. Reports Page
- Exercise summary with all statistics
- Nebenstationen breakdown (auto-calculated)
- Per-repeater breakdown
- Export buttons: TXT (Bund format), TXT (Land format), Combined, PDF

#### 6. Operator Registry Page (Admin only)
- Searchable/filterable list of all operators
- Bulk import from Excel
- Individual edit with full field set
- Operator history: all exercises they participated in

### Seed Data

Create a seed script that populates:

1. **All 9 Austrian Bundesländer** + international entries (Slowenien, CZ, Deutschland, Italien, Slowakei, Kroatien, Liechtenstein, CH, Ungarn)

2. **All Austrian Bezirke** — use this definitive list derived from both prototypes:
   - Wien: W (Wien)
   - Salzburg: HA (Hallein), JO (St. Johann), SC (Salzburg Stadt), SL (Salzburg-Umgebung), TA (Tamsweg), ZE (Zell am See)
   - NÖ: AM, BN, BL, GF, GD, HL, HO, KO, KS, KR, LF, ME, MI, MD, NK, PL, SB, TU, WT, WN, WB, ZT
   - Burgenland: EU, GS, JE, MA, ND, OP, OW
   - OÖ: BR, EF, FR, GM, GR, KI, LL, PE, RI, RO, SD, SR, SE, UU, VB, WE, WL
   - Steiermark: BM, DL, GU, HF, LB, LE, LI, MU, MT, SO, VO, WZ
   - Tirol: IM, IL, KU, LZ, RE, SZ
   - Kärnten: FE, HE, KC (Klagenfurt Stadt), KL, SV, SP, VI, VL, VK, WO
   - Vorarlberg: DO, FK, BC (Bregenz)

3. **Repeaters** with full technical details:
   - Gerlitze 2m: OE8XNK, 145.7625 MHz, -0.6, BURST 1750 Hz
   - Gerlitze 70cm: 439.050, -7.6, CTCSS 88.5 Hz
   - Magdalensberg linked (OE8XMK): 438.575, -7.6, CTCSS 88.5 Hz, is_linked=true

4. **Einstiegspunkte** for the Magdalensberg linked system:
   - Hermannskogel OE1XAT (Wien) → "HK"
   - Gaisberg OE2XZR (Salzburg) → "GB"
   - Nebelstein OE3XNR (NÖ) → "NBST"

   - Telfs OE7XKG (Tirol) → "Telfs"
   - Hochstein OE7XLI (Tirol) → "Hochstein"
   - Ahorn OE7XZT (Tirol) → "Ahorn"
   - Magdalensberg OE8XMK (Kärnten) → "MK"
   - Dornbirn OE9XXD (Vorarlberg) → "DO"

5. **Default admin:** OE8YML, PIN: changeme, role: admin

### Data Import

Build an import endpoint/script that reads the existing Excel workbook and imports:
- All ~1,066 operators with their full profile data
- Historical exercise data from the 6 completed weekly sheets
- Map the Excel Bezirks-Kenner codes to the unified Bezirk reference table

Use the `xlsx` npm package for reading the Excel file.

---

### Docker Setup

```dockerfile
# Multi-stage build
FROM node:20-alpine AS builder
WORKDIR /app
COPY package*.json ./
COPY client/package*.json ./client/
COPY server/package*.json ./server/
RUN npm ci
COPY . .
RUN npm run build

FROM node:20-alpine AS production
WORKDIR /app
COPY --from=builder /app/dist ./dist
COPY --from=builder /app/node_modules ./node_modules
COPY --from=builder /app/package.json ./
VOLUME /data
ENV DATABASE_PATH=/data/bosarsalog.db
EXPOSE 3000
CMD ["node", "dist/server/index.js"]
```

```yaml
# docker-compose.yml
services:
  bosarsalog:
    build: .
    container_name: bosarsalog
    restart: unless-stopped
    ports:
      - "3000:3000"
    volumes:
      - bosarsalog-data:/data
    environment:
      - NODE_ENV=production
      - DATABASE_PATH=/data/bosarsalog.db
      - JWT_SECRET=${JWT_SECRET}

  cloudflared:
    image: cloudflare/cloudflared:latest
    container_name: bosarsalog-tunnel
    restart: unless-stopped
    profiles:
      - tunnel
    command: tunnel run
    environment:
      - TUNNEL_TOKEN=${TUNNEL_TOKEN}
    depends_on:
      - bosarsalog

volumes:
  bosarsalog-data:
```

### Implementation Order

Build in this sequence:

1. **Project scaffolding** — monorepo, CLAUDE.md, package.json, tsconfig, Docker
2. **Database schema + migrations** — SQLite setup with better-sqlite3
3. **Seed script** — all reference data (Bundesländer, Bezirke, Repeaters, Einstiegspunkte)
4. **Auth** — admin table, login endpoint, JWT middleware
5. **Operator CRUD** — registry with search/autocomplete
6. **Exercise CRUD** — create, configure repeaters, status management
7. **Signal Report CRUD** — the core data entry API
8. **Socket.IO real-time** — broadcast report changes to all connected admins
9. **Frontend: Login + Dashboard** — basic navigation shell
10. **Frontend: Live Entry (Land mode)** — repeater-first entry, keyboard shortcuts
11. **Frontend: Live Entry (Bund mode)** — geography-first entry with Einstiegspunkt
12. **Frontend: Running totals + live sync** — real-time stats bar
13. **Reports + Export** — Gesamtübersicht, Nebenstationen, TXT exports
14. **Excel import** — bulk import from existing workbook
15. **Operator registry UI** — admin management page
16. **Docker + deployment** — production build, compose, tunnel

---

### Key UX Details to Get Right

- **Speed is everything.** During a live exercise, admins enter ~180 reports in ~60 minutes. Every keystroke matters. Autofocus, enter-to-submit, auto-uppercase, keyboard shortcuts.
- **Einstiegspunkt auto-propagation:** When admin selects "Hermannskogel" in the WC (Wien) Bezirk, auto-set it for all other Wien Bezirke too. This was implemented in the existing Bund prototype and must be preserved.
- **Cross-repeater sync:** When adding OE8PPK to Gerlitze 2m, auto-create empty placeholder entries for OE8PPK on Goldeck, Direkte, etc. This was implemented in the existing Land prototype.
- **The warm beige background** (#faf3e4) from both prototypes is intentional — don't change it to cold corporate blue. Keep the warm, approachable feel.
- **Badges:** Bezirk codes shown as badges — Hauptstadt districts with red (#dc3545), others with gray (#6c757d). Bundesland headers with blue badges.
- **Running totals always visible:** Fixed bar showing "Anwesend: 125 | Rapporte: 179 | Gerlitze: 29 | Magdalensberg: 109 | ..."

---

### What NOT to Build (Out of Scope for MVP)

- Mobile native app (responsive web is sufficient)
- Offline mode with sync (nice-to-have for later)
- Map visualization / geographic heat map
- Integration with safeReach / Meshtastic / FunkPilot
- Per-operator trend analysis
- SSB/HF exercise mode (the SSB/Erdbeben columns in Excel are unused)

```json
{
  "file_path": "/home/achildrenmile/bosarsalog/CLAUDE.md",
  "content": "# BOS-ARSA Log — BOS-ARSA Krisenkommunikationsübung\n\n## What is this?\nA real-time multi-user web app for weekly ham radio crisis communication exercises.\nAdmins log signal reports from amateur radio operators across Austria on multiple\nrepeaters and frequencies. Replaces a shared Excel workbook + two HTML prototypes.\n\n## Tech Stack\n- Frontend: React 18, TypeScript, Vite, Tailwind CSS, Socket.IO client\n- Backend: Node.js, Express, TypeScript, Socket.IO server\n- Database: SQLite via better-sqlite3\n- Auth: Simple callsign + PIN token auth\n- Deployment: Docker, Cloudflare Tunnel\n\n## Architecture\n- Monorepo: /client (React SPA) + /server (Express API + WebSocket)\n- RESTful API for CRUD, WebSocket for real-time sync\n- SQLite DB file at /data/bosarsalog.db (Docker volume mounted)\n- Server serves the built client in production\n\n## Key Domain Concepts\n- Operator (Rufzeichen): ham radio callsign, e.g. OE8YML, S55UJE\n- Repeater/Frequency (Umsetzer): radio relay station with TX freq, offset, CTCSS\n- Linked Repeater: one logical repeater spanning multiple physical sites (Einstiegspunkte)\n- Einstiegspunkt: physical entry point in a linked repeater network\n- Signal Report (Rapport): RST format e.g. \"5/9+30 HK\" = R5/S9+30dB via Hermannskogel\n- Exercise (Übung): one Sunday session with configured repeaters and collected reports\n- Bezirk: Austrian political district, identified by 2-3 letter code\n- Bundesland: Austrian state (OE1=Wien through OE9=Vorarlberg)\n\n## Conventions\n- German UI text, English code\n- Callsigns always uppercase\n- RST format: R/S with optional +dB modifier and location suffix\n- All timestamps in Europe/Vienna timezone\n- API prefix: /api/v1/\n\n## Common Commands\n- `npm run dev` — start both client and server in dev mode\n- `npm run build` — build for production\n- `npm run db:seed` — seed database with reference data\n- `docker compose up -d` — production deployment\n"
}
```

{
  "file_path": "/home/achildrenmile/bosarsalog/client/src/App.tsx",
  "content": "import { Routes, Route, Navigate } from 'react-router-dom';\nimport { AuthProvider, useAuth } from './hooks/useAuth';\nimport Layout from './components/Layout';\nimport LoginPage from './pages/LoginPage';\nimport DashboardPage from './pages/DashboardPage';\nimport ExercisePage from './pages/ExercisePage';\nimport ExerciseSetupPage from './pages/ExerciseSetupPage';\nimport OperatorsPage from './pages/OperatorsPage';\nimport ReportsPage from './pages/ReportsPage';\n\nfunction ProtectedRoute({ children }: { children: React.ReactNode }) {\n  const { token } = useAuth();\n  if (!token) return <Navigate to=\"/login\" replace />;\n  return <>{children}</>;\n}\n\nexport default function App() {\n  return (\n    <AuthProvider>\n      <Routes>\n        <Route path=\"/login\" element={<LoginPage />} />\n        <Route\n          path=\"/\"\n          element={\n            <ProtectedRoute>\n              <Layout />\n            </ProtectedRoute>\n          }\n        >\n          <Route index element={<DashboardPage />} />\n          <Route path=\"exercises/:id\" element={<ExercisePage />} />\n          <Route path=\"exercises/:id/setup\" element={<ExerciseSetupPage />} />\n          <Route path=\"exercises/:id/reports\" element={<ReportsPage />} />\n          <Route path=\"operators\" element={<OperatorsPage />} />\n        </Route>\n      </Routes>\n    </AuthProvider>\n  );\n}\n"
}

## Comprehensive BandBlick Deployment Infrastructure Report

I've thoroughly explored the `/home/achildrenmile/bandblick/` directory and examined all deployment-related files. Here's the complete breakdown:

---

### 1. DEPLOY SCRIPT OVERVIEW (`deploy-production.sh`)

**Location:** `/home/achildrenmile/bandblick/deploy-production.sh`

The deployment script is a comprehensive bash automation tool with the following characteristics:

**Key Features:**
- **Color-coded output** for better readability (RED for errors, GREEN for success, YELLOW for warnings)
- **Environment validation**: Requires `.env.production` file with mandatory variables
- **Required variables validation**: Checks for `SYNOLOGY_HOST`, `REMOTE_DIR`, `CONTAINER_NAME`, `IMAGE_NAME`, `CONTAINER_PORT`, and `SITE_URL`
- **Rebuild support**: Accepts `--rebuild` flag to build Docker images without cache

**Four-Step Deployment Process:**

1. **Sync Code to Synology** (SSH-based)
   - Checks if remote directory exists
   - If exists: performs `git pull` to update code
   - If not exists: clones the repository from origin

2. **Build Docker Image** (on Synology)
   - Uses Synology-specific Docker path: `/var/packages/Docker/target/usr/bin/docker`
   - Builds image with name from `IMAGE_NAME` variable
   - Respects `--rebuild` flag for cache-less builds

3. **Restart Container**
   - Stops and removes existing container (with error suppression for non-existent containers)
   - Starts new container with comprehensive configuration:
     - Auto-restart policy: `unless-stopped`
     - Environment variables for site branding
     - Comprehensive health checks:
       - Command: `wget --no-verbose --tries=1 --spider http://localhost:80/ || exit 1`
       - Interval: 30 seconds
       - Timeout: 10 seconds
       - Retries: 3 attempts
       - Start period: 10 seconds (wait before first health check)

4. **Verification**
   - Waits 3 seconds for container startup
   - Checks container status via `docker ps`
   - Tests local port accessibility (port 80)
   - Attempts to verify public URL accessibility
   - Displays warning if Cloudflare tunnel not yet configured

---

### 2. DOCKER-COMPOSE STRUCTURE

**Location:** `/home/achildrenmile/bandblick/docker-compose.yml`

Simple, streamlined configuration for local development:

```yaml
services:
  bandblick:
    build: .                           # Builds from local Dockerfile
    ports:
      - "127.0.0.1:3402:80"           # Local dev port mapping
    restart: unless-stopped
    environment:
      - SITE_NAME=BandBlick
      - SITE_DESCRIPTION=IARU Region 1 Band Plan Explorer
      - PARENT_SITE_NAME=OE Radio
      - PARENT_SITE_URL=https://oeradio.at
      - PARENT_SITE_LOGO=https://oeradio.at/wp-content/uploads/2026/01/oeradiologo-300x200.png
```

**Key Points:**
- Localhost binding (127.0.0.1) for security - only accessible locally
- Port 3402 for local development
- Runtime configuration via environment variables for branding
- No external dependencies defined (no databases, services, etc.)

---

### 3. DOCKERFILE (Multi-Stage Build)

**Location:** `/home/achildrenmile/bandblick/Dockerfile`

Uses efficient multi-stage Docker build pattern:

**Stage 1: Builder (node:20-alpine)**
- Installs dependencies from `package*.json`
- Uses `npm ci` for reproducible builds
- Copies source code and runs `npm run build`
- Output: `/app/dist` directory

**Stage 2: Runtime (nginx:alpine)**
- Minimal nginx-based container
- Installs `wget` for health checks
- Copies built files from builder to `/usr/share/nginx/html`
- Copies custom `nginx.conf` configuration
- Copies and executes `docker-entrypoint.sh` script
- Exposes port 80
- Entrypoint: `/docker-entrypoint.sh` (dynamic configuration script)

**Image size optimization:**
- Alpine base images (lightweight)
- Multi-stage build avoids shipping build tools
- No node modules or dev dependencies in final image

---

### 4. NGINX CONFIGURATION (`nginx.conf`)

**Location:** `/home/achildrenmile/bandblick/nginx.conf`

Sophisticated configuration for SPA (Single Page Application) serving with intelligent caching:

**Gzip Compression:**
- Enabled with minimum 1KB threshold
- Applies to: text/plain, text/css, text/xml, text/javascript, application/javascript, application/json, application/xml

**Routing Strategy:**
```
location / - SPA routing with fallback to index.html
  - Serves static files if they exist
  - Falls back to index.html for client-side routing
  - No-cache headers (prevents browser cache)
  
location ~* \.json$ - Config files (runtime configuration)
  - No-cache headers (config.json generated at runtime)
  
location ~* \.(js|css)$ - Static assets with hash in filename
  - Cache for 1 year (immutable)
  - Only safe because Vite/build process adds content hash to filenames
  
location ~* \.(ico|svg|png|jpg|jpeg|gif|webp|woff|woff2|ttf|eot)$ - Images and fonts
  - Cache for 7 days
  - Allows browser caching for performance
```

**Cache Control Headers:**
- HTML/JSON: `no-cache, no-store, must-revalidate`
- Hashed assets: `public, immutable` with 1-year expiration
- Images/fonts: `public` with 7-day expiration

---

### 5. DOCKER ENTRYPOINT SCRIPT (`docker-entrypoint.sh`)

**Location:** `/home/achildrenmile/bandblick/docker-entrypoint.sh`

Generates runtime configuration and starts nginx:

```sh
#!/bin/sh
set -e

# Generates /usr/share/nginx/html/config.json from environment variables
# Structure:
# {
#   "siteName": "${SITE_NAME:-BandBlick}",
#   "siteDescription": "${SITE_DESCRIPTION:-IARU Region 1 Band Plan Explorer}",
#   "parentSiteName": "${PARENT_SITE_NAME:-}",
#   "parentSiteUrl": "${PARENT_SITE_URL:-}",
#   "parentSiteLogo": "${PARENT_SITE_LOGO:-}"
# }

# Starts nginx in foreground (required for Docker container to stay running)
exec nginx -g "daemon off;"
```

**Key Features:**
- Provides flexible runtime branding configuration
- Outputs generated config to stdout for debugging
- Runs nginx in foreground so container lifecycle is tied to nginx process

---

### COMPLETE DEPLOYMENT WORKFLOW

```
Local Development:
  npm install → npm run dev (Vite dev server on :5173)
  OR
  docker-compose up (local dev on 127.0.0.1:3402)

Production Deployment:
  1. Developer runs: ./deploy-production.sh [--rebuild]
  2. Script validates .env.production and required vars
  3. SSH to Synology: git pull (or git clone if first time)
  4. Build Docker image on Synology (Node.js → npm build → Nginx image)
  5. Stop old container, start new container with health checks
  6. Container runs entrypoint.sh → generates config.json → starts nginx
  7. Nginx serves static SPA files with intelligent caching
  8. Cloudflared (separate process) tunnels port 3406 → bandblick.oeradio.at
  9. Verification: curl localhost:3406 and public URL

Health Monitoring:
  - Docker health check every 30s via wget to port 80
  - Automatically restarts if 3 consecutive failures
  - Container auto-restarts on Synology reboot
```

---

### KEY ARCHITECTURAL INSIGHTS

1. **Synology-First Design**: Deployment optimized for Synology NAS with custom Docker path
2. **SSH-Driven**: All deployment operations via SSH, no agent/CD pipeline visible
3. **Environment-Based Configuration**: Runtime branding via environment variables
4. **Health-Conscious**: Multiple layers of health checking (Docker health checks, curl verification)
5. **Cloudflare Integration**: Public internet access via Cloudflare tunnel (not directly exposed)
6. **Cache-Smart**: Intelligent caching strategy leveraging content-hashing
7. **Zero-Downtime Philosophy**: Old container stops before new one starts (brief downtime acceptable)
8. **Minimal Dependencies**: Single Docker container, no external services in compose file

All relevant files are located in:
- `/home/achildrenmile/bandblick/deploy-production.sh`
- `/home/achildrenmile/bandblick/docker-compose.yml`
- `/home/achildrenmile/bandblick/Dockerfile`
- `/home/achildrenmile/bandblick/docker-entrypoint.sh`
- `/home/achildrenmile/bandblick/nginx.conf`
- `/home/achildrenmile/bandblick/.env.production`
- `/home/achildrenmile/bandblick/.env.production.example`

Each tunnel uses a `TUNNEL_TOKEN` environment variable from a `.env` file and connects to a shared external Docker network called **`cloudflared-tunnel`**.

All three compose files at `/home/achildrenmile/infra/services/cloudflared*/docker-compose.yml` follow the same pattern:
```yaml
services:
  cloudflared-xxx:
    image: cloudflare/cloudflared:latest
    container_name: cloudflared-xxx
    restart: unless-stopped
    command: tunnel run
    environment:
      - TUNNEL_TOKEN=${TUNNEL_TOKEN}
    networks:
      - cloudflared-tunnel

networks:
  cloudflared-tunnel:
    external: true
```

---

## 4. Domain/Tunnel Configuration Pattern

The domain routing is configured **on the Cloudflare dashboard side** (not in config files on the host). Each tunnel token embeds which hostnames route to which local services. The containers just run `tunnel run` with the token.

Apps join the shared **`cloudflared-tunnel`** Docker network (which is external/pre-created), allowing the cloudflared containers to reach them by container name. For example, CRISP's compose file shows:
```yaml
networks:
  cloudflared-tunnel:
    external: true
```

---

## 5. Bosarsalog Directory

**No `bosarsalog` directory exists on the remote host.** The home directory contains:
- `~/apps/` -- application deployments (crisp, irsiglerdach)
- `~/commonhall/` -- the CommonHall project
- `~/infra/` -- infrastructure services (cloudflared tunnels, umami, uptime-kuma, etc.)
- `~/docker/` -- (likely Docker config/data)

---

## Key Takeaways for Deploying Bosarsalog

1. **Create a new Cloudflare tunnel** on the Cloudflare dashboard for bosarsalog (or add a hostname to the existing `cloudflared` tunnel).
2. **Follow the established pattern**: create a service directory (either under `~/infra/services/bosarsalog/` or `~/apps/bosarsalog/`) with a `docker-compose.yml` that joins the `cloudflared-tunnel` external network.
3. If a dedicated tunnel is needed, add a new `~/infra/services/cloudflared-bosarsalog/` directory with its own compose file and tunnel token.
4. The Docker network `cloudflared-tunnel` already exists and is ready to use.

### oeradio.at WordPress Stack
Located at `/volume1/docker/oeradioat/docker-compose.yml`, it runs:
- **WordPress** on port `127.0.0.1:3081` (this is what the cloudflared tunnel routes `oeradio.at` to)
- **MariaDB 11.3** as the database
- **Redis 7** for caching
- All on a bridge network called `oeradioat-network`

### Key Takeaways

1. **There is already a fully configured tunnel for oeradio.at** -- tunnel ID `f1f976e0-da63-48e7-b66b-9aedfb5dbe87`, running in the `cloudflared-oeradio` container.
2. The tunnel handles 24 subdomains of oeradio.at, routing to various services on localhost ports 3005-3415.
3. The architecture pattern is: each tunnel is a standalone `docker run` container with host networking, mounting a config directory from `/volume1/docker/cloudflared-<name>/` containing `config.yml` and a credentials JSON file.
4. To add a new subdomain to the oeradio.at tunnel, you would edit `/volume1/docker/cloudflared-oeradio/config.yml`, add the ingress rule, create the corresponding CNAME DNS record pointing to `f1f976e0-da63-48e7-b66b-9aedfb5dbe87.cfargotunnel.com`, and restart the container.
5. The next available port in the oeradio.at range appears to be **3416** (the current highest is 3415 for funkpilot).

role: string;\n  };\n}\n\nexport function authMiddleware(req: Request, res: Response, next: NextFunction): void {\n  const header = req.headers.authorization;\n  if (!header || !header.startsWith('Bearer ')) {\n    res.status(401).json({ error: 'Nicht autorisiert' });\n    return;\n  }\n  try {\n    const token = header.slice(7);\n    const payload = jwt.verify(token, res.status(401).json({ error: 'Token ungültig' });\n  }\n}\n\nexport function requireRole(...roles: string[]) {\n  return (req: Request, res: Response, next: NextFunction): void => {\n    const admin = (req as AuthRequest).admin;\n    if (!admin || !roles.includes(admin.role)) {\n      res.status(403).json({ error: 'Keine Berechtigung' });\n      return;\n    }\n    next();\n  };\n}\n\nexport { const { callsign, pin } = req.body;\n  if (!callsign || !pin) {\n    res.status(400).json({ error: 'Rufzeichen und PIN erforderlich' });\n    return;\n  }\n\n  const db = getDb();\n  const admin = db.prepare('SELECT * FROM admins WHERE callsign = ?').get(callsign.toUpperCase()) as any;\n  if (!admin || !bcrypt.compareSync(pin, admin.pin_hash)) {\n    res.status(401).json({ error: 'Rufzeichen oder PIN ungültig' });\n    return;\n  }\n\n  const token = jwt.sign(\n    { id: admin.id, callsign: admin.callsign, role: admin.role },\n    JWT_SECRET,\n    { expiresIn: '24h' }\n  );\n\n '../middleware/auth.js';\n\nexport function setupSocket(io: Server): void {\n  io.use((socket, next) => {\n    const token = socket.handshake.auth?.token;\n    if (!token) return next(new Error('Nicht autorisiert'));\n    try {\n      const payload = jwt.verify(token, JWT_SECRET) as any;\n      (socket as any).admin = payload;\n      next();\n    } catch {\n      next(new Error('Token ungültig'));\n    }\n  });\n\n  io.on('connection', (socket: Socket) => {\n    const admin = (socket as any).admin;\n    console.log(`Socket connected: ${admin.callsign}`);\n\n    socket.on('join_exercise', ({ exercise_id }) => {\n      socket.join(`exercise:${exercise_id}`);\n      console.log(`${admin.callsign} joined exercise ${exercise_id}`);\n    });\n\n    socket.on('leave_exercise', ({ exercise_id }) => {\n      socket.leave(`exercise:${exercise_id}`);\n    });\n\n    socket.on('report_created', (data) => {\n      socket.to(`exercise:${data.exercise_id}`).emit('report_created', {\n        ...data,\n        entered_by: admin.callsign,\n      });\n    });\n\n    socket.on('report_updated', (data) => {\n      socket.to(`exercise:${data.exercise_id}`).emit('report_updated', {\n        ...data,\n        entered_by: admin.callsign,\n      });\n    });\n\n    socket.on('report_deleted', (data) => {\n      socket.to(`exercise:${data.exercise_id}`).emit('report_deleted', {\n        ...data,\n        entered_by: admin.callsign,\n      });\n    });\n\n    socket.on('attendance_updated', (data) => {\n      socket.to(`exercise:${data.exercise_id}`).emit('attendance_updated', {\n        ...data,\n        entered_by: admin.callsign,\n      });\n    });\n\n    socket.on('disconnect', () => {\n      console.log(`Socket disconnected: ${admin.callsign}`);\n    });\n  });\n}\n```\n\nUse cat with heredoc to create each file."

`).all();\n  res.json(exercises);\n});\n\n// Create exercise\nexercisesRouter.post('/', (req, res) => {\n  const { date } = req.body;\n  if (!date) {\n    res.status(400).json({ error: 'Datum exercises WHERE id = ?').get(result.lastInsertRowid);\n    res.status(201).json(exercise);\n  } catch (e: any) {\n    if (e.message?.includes('UNIQUE')) {\n      res.status(409).json({ error: 'Übung exercise = db.prepare('SELECT * FROM exercises WHERE id = ?').get(req.params.id);\n  if (!exercise) {\n    res.status(404).json({ error: 'Übung nicht gefunden' });\n    return;\n  }\n\n  const repeaters = db.prepare(`\n    SELECT er.*, r.short_name, r.site_name, r.band, r.callsign as repeater_callsign,\n      r.frequency_mhz, r.offset_mhz, r.ctcss_hz, r.burst_hz, r.type, r.is_linked\n AND sr.is_op_marker = 0\n    ORDER BY bl.sort_order, bz.code, o.callsign\n  `).all(req.params.id) as any[];\n\n  let text = `BOS-ARSA Krisenkommunikationsübung — ${exercise.date}\\n`;\n  text += `Auswertung nach Bundesland\\n`;\n  text += '='.repeat(60) + '\\n\\n';\n\n  let currentBl = '';\n  let currentBz = '';\n  for (const r of reports) {\n    if (r.bundesland_name !== currentBl) {\n      currentBl = r.bundesland_name || 'Sonstige';\n      text += `\\n── ${currentBl} ──\\n`;\n      currentBz = '';\n    }\n    if (r.bezirk_code !== currentBz) {\n      currentBz = r.bezirk_code || '??';\n      text += `  [${currentBz}] ${r.bezirk_name || ''}\\n`;\n    }\n    const rapport = r.readability && r.strength\n      ? `${r.readability}/${r.strength}${r.db_over_s9 || ''}`\n      : '—';\n    const ep = r.ep_abbr ? ` ${r.ep_abbr}` : '';\n    text += `    ${r.callsign.padEnd(10)} ${r.repeater_name.padEnd(20)} ${rapport}${ep}\\n`;\n  }\n\n  res.setHeader('Content-Type', 'text/plain; charset=utf-8');\n  res.setHeader('Content-Disposition', `attachment; filename=\"bos-arsa-bund-${exercise.date}.txt\"`);\n  res.send(text);\n});\n\n// Land export (Repeater-grouped)\nexportRouter.get('/exercises/:id/land', (req, res) => {\n  const db = getDb();\n  const exercise = db.prepare('SELECT * FROM exercises WHERE id = ?').get(req.params.id) as any;\n  if (!exercise) { res.status(404).json({ error: 'Nicht gefunden' }); return; }\n\n  const reports = db.prepare(`\n    SELECT sr.*, o.callsign, o.bezirk_code, o.bundesland_code,\n      bl.name as bundesland_name, bz.name as bezirk_name,\n      r.short_name as repeater_name, r.sort_order as rep_sort,\n      ep.abbreviation as ep_abbr\n    FROM signal_reports sr\n    JOIN operators o ON o.id = sr.operator_id\n    JOIN repeaters r ON r.id = sr.repeater_id\n    LEFT JOIN bundeslaender bl ON bl.code = o.bundesland_code\n    LEFT JOIN bezirke bz ON bz.code = o.bezirk_code\n    LEFT JOIN einstiegspunkte ep ON ep.id = sr.einstiegspunkt_id\n    WHERE sr.exercise_id = ? AND sr.is_op_marker = 0\n    ORDER BY r.sort_order, bl.sort_order, bz.code, o.callsign\n  `).all(req.params.id) as any[];\n\n  let text = `BOS-ARSA Krisenkommunikationsübung — ${exercise.date}\\n`;\n  text += `Auswertung nach Umsetzer\\n`;\n  text += '='.repeat(60) + '\\n\\n';\n\n  let currentRep = '';\n  let currentBz = '';\n  for (const r of reports) {\n    if (r.repeater_name !== currentRep) {\n      currentRep = r.repeater_name;\n      text += `\\n══ ${currentRep} ══\\n`;\n      currentBz = '';\n    }\n    if (r.bezirk_code !== currentBz) {\n      currentBz = r.bezirk_code || '??';\n      text += `  [${currentBz}] ${r.bezirk_name || ''}\\n`;\n    }\n    const rapport = r.readability && r.strength\n      ? `${r.readability}/${r.strength}${r.db_over_s9 || ''}`\n      : '—';\n    const ep = r.ep_abbr ? ` ${r.ep_abbr}` : '';\n    text += `    ${r.callsign.padEnd(10)} ${rapport}${ep}\\n`;\n  }\n\n  res.setHeader('Content-Type', 'text/plain; charset=utf-8');\n  res.setHeader('Content-Disposition', `attachment; filename=\"bos-arsa-land-${exercise.date}.txt\"`);\n  res.send(text);\n});\n\n// Combined export\nexportRouter.get('/exercises/:id/combined', (req, res) => {\n  const db = getDb();\n  const exercise = db.prepare('SELECT * FROM exercises WHERE id = ?').get(req.params.id) as any;\n  if (!exercise) { res.status(404).json({ error: 'Nicht gefunden' }); return; }\n\n  const stats = db.prepare(`\n    SELECT\n      (SELECT COUNT(DISTINCT operator_id) FROM exercise_attendance WHERE exercise_id = ? AND is_present = 1) as participants,\n      (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM signal_reports WHERE exercise_id = ? AND is_op_marker = 0) as reports\n  `).get(req.params.id, req.params.id) as any;\n\n  const perRepeater = db.prepare(`\n    SELECT r.short_name, COUNT(*) as count\n    FROM signal_reports sr\n    JOIN repeaters r ON r.id = sr.repeater_id\n    WHERE sr.exercise_id = ? AND sr.is_op_marker = 0\n    GROUP BY sr.repeater_id ORDER BY r.sort_order\n  `).all(req.params.id) as any[];\n\n  let text = `BOS-ARSA Krisenkommunikationsübung — ${exercise.date}\\n`;\n  text += '='.repeat(60) + '\\n';\n  text += `Teilnehmer: ${stats.participants}\\n`;\n  text += `Rapporte gesamt: ${stats.reports}\\n\\n`;\n  text += 'Pro Umsetzer:\\n';\n  for (const r of perRepeater) {\n    text += `  ${r.short_name.padEnd(25)} ${r.count}\\n`;\n  }\n  text += '\\n';\n\n  res.setHeader('Content-Type', 'text/plain; charset=utf-8');\n  res.setHeader('Content-Disposition', `attachment; filename=\"bos-arsa-combined-${exercise.date}.txt\"`);\n  res.send(text);\n});\n```\n\n7. /home/achildrenmile/bosarsalog/server/src/db/reset.ts:\n```typescript\nimport fs from 'fs';\nimport path from 'path';\nimport { fileURLToPath } from 'url';\n\nconst __dirname = path.dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url));\nconst DB_PATH = process.env.DATABASE_PATH || path.join(__dirname, '..', '..', '..', 'data', 'bosarsalog.db');\n\nif (fs.existsSync(DB_PATH)) {\n  fs.unlinkSync(DB_PATH);\n  console.log('Database deleted:', DB_PATH);\n} else {\n  console.log('No database found at:', DB_PATH);\n}\n```\n\nUse cat with heredoc for each file."

  "content": "import { io, Socket } from 'socket.io-client';\n\nlet socket: Socket | null = null;\n\nexport function getSocket(): Socket {\n  if (!socket) {\n    const token = localStorage.getItem('token');\n    socket = io('/', { auth: { token } });\n  }\n  return socket;\n}\n\nexport function disconnectSocket(): void {\n  if (socket) {\n    socket.disconnect();\n    socket = null;\n  }\n}\n"
}

{
  "file_path": "/home/achildrenmile/bosarsalog/client/src/pages/ExercisePage.tsx",
  "content": "import { useState, useEffect, useCallback, useRef } from 'react';\nimport { useParams, Link } from 'react-router-dom';\nimport { apiFetch } from '../services/api';\nimport { getSocket } from '../services/socket';\nimport { useAuth } from '../hooks/useAuth';\nimport LandMode from '../components/LandMode';\nimport BundMode from '../components/BundMode';\nimport RunningTotals from '../components/RunningTotals';\n\ninterface Exercise {\n  id: number;\n  date: string;\n  status: string;\n  notes: string;\n  repeaters: any[];\n  reports: any[];\n  attendance: any[];\n}\n\ninterface Stats {\n  totalParticipants: number;\n  totalReports: number;\n  perRepeater: { short_name: string; count: number }[];\n}\n\nexport default function ExercisePage() {\n  const { id } = useParams<{ id: string }>();\n  const { admin } = useAuth();\n  const [exercise, setExercise] = useState<Exercise | null>(null);\n  const [mode, setMode] = useState<'land' | 'bund'>('land');\n  const [stats, setStats] = useState<Stats>({ totalParticipants: 0, totalReports: 0, perRepeater: [] });\n  const [reports, setReports] = useState<any[]>([]);\n  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);\n  const socketRef = useRef(getSocket());\n\n  const refreshStats = useCallback(async () => {\n    if (!id) return;\n    try {\n      const s = await apiFetch(`/api/v1/exercises/${id}/stats`);\n      setStats(s);\n    } catch {}\n  }, [id]);\n\n  const refreshReports = useCallback(async () => {\n    if (!id) return;\n    try {\n      const r = await apiFetch(`/api/v1/exercises/${id}/reports`);\n      setReports(r);\n    } catch {}\n  }, [id]);\n\n  useEffect(() => {\n    if (!id) return;\n    setLoading(true);\n    apiFetch(`/api/v1/exercises/${id}`)\n      .then(data => {\n        setExercise(data);\n        setReports(data.reports || []);\n      })\n      .catch(() => {})\n      .finally(() => setLoading(false));\n    refreshStats();\n  }, [id, refreshStats]);\n\n  // Socket.IO setup\n  useEffect(() => {\n    if (!id) return;\n    const socket = socketRef.current;\n    socket.emit('join_exercise', { exercise_id: parseInt(id) });\n\n    socket.on('report_created', (data: any) => {\n      setReports(prev => [data, ...prev]);\n      refreshStats();\n    });\n    socket.on('report_updated', (data: any) => {\n      setReports(prev => prev.map(r => r.id === data.id ? data : r));\n      refreshStats();\n    });\n    socket.on('report_deleted', (data: any) => {\n      setReports(prev => prev.filter(r => r.id !== data.report_id));\n      refreshStats();\n    });\n\n    return () => {\n      socket.emit('leave_exercise', { exercise_id: parseInt(id) });\n      socket.off('report_created');\n      socket.off('report_updated');\n      socket.off('report_deleted');\n    };\n  }, [id, refreshStats]);\n\n  const handleReportCreated = useCallback(async (report: any) => {\n    setReports(prev => [report, ...prev]);\n    socketRef.current.emit('report_created', { ...report, exercise_id: parseInt(id!) });\n    refreshStats();\n  }, [id, refreshStats]);\n\n  const handleReportUpdated = useCallback(async (report: any) => {\n    setReports(prev => prev.map(r => r.id === report.id ? report : r));\n    socketRef.current.emit('report_updated', { ...report, exercise_id: parseInt(id!) });\n    refreshStats();\n  }, [id, refreshStats]);\n\n  const handleReportDeleted = useCallback(async (reportId: number) => {\n    setReports(prev => prev.filter(r => r.id !== reportId));\n    socketRef.current.emit('report_deleted', { report_id: reportId, exercise_id: parseInt(id!) });\n    refreshStats();\n  }, [id, refreshStats]);\n\n  const handleStatusChange = async (newStatus: string) => {\n    if (!id) return;\n    await apiFetch(`/api/v1/exercises/${id}`, {\n      method: 'PATCH',\n      body: JSON.stringify({ status: newStatus }),\n    });\n    setExercise(prev => prev ? { ...prev, status: newStatus } : null);\n  };\n\n  if (loading) return <p className=\"text-gray-500 p-4\">Laden...</p>;\n  if (!exercise) return <p className=\"text-red-500 p-4\">Übung nicht gefunden</p>;\n\n  const statusColor = exercise.status === 'active' ? 'bg-green-500' : exercise.status === 'completed' ? 'bg-blue-500' : 'bg-gray-400';\n  const statusLabel = exercise.status === 'active' ? 'Aktiv' : exercise.status === 'completed' ? 'Abgeschlossen' : 'Geplant';\n\n  return (\n    <div className=\"space-y-4\">\n      {/* Header */}\n      <div className=\"flex items-center justify-between\">\n        <div className=\"flex items-center gap-4\">\n          <h1 className=\"text-xl font-bold text-[#5b3a1a]\">\n            Übung {new Date(exercise.date + 'T00:00:00').toLocaleDateString('de-AT', { day: '2-digit', month: '2-digit', year: 'numeric' })}\n          </h1>\n          <span className={`px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full text-xs text-white ${statusColor}`}>{statusLabel}</span>\n        </div>\n        <div className=\"flex items-center gap-2\">\n          {exercise.status === 'planned' && (\n            <button onClick={() => handleStatusChange('active')} className=\"bg-green-600 hover:bg-green-700 text-white px-3 py-1 rounded text-sm\">\n              Starten\n            </button>\n          )}\n          {exercise.status === 'active' && (\n            <button onClick={() => handleStatusChange('completed')} className=\"bg-blue-600 hover:bg-blue-700 text-white px-3 py-1 rounded text-sm\">\n              Abschließen\n            </button>\n          )}\n          <Link to={`/exercises/${id}/setup`} className=\"bg-gray-200 hover:bg-gray-300 text-gray-700 px-3 py-1 rounded text-sm\">\n            Einrichten\n          </Link>\n          <Link to={`/exercises/${id}/reports`} className=\"bg-amber-100 hover:bg-amber-200 text-amber-800 px-3 py-1 rounded text-sm\">\n            Auswertung\n          </Link>\n        </div>\n      </div>\n\n      {/* Running totals */}\n      <RunningTotals stats={stats} />\n\n      {/* Mode tabs */}\n      <div className=\"flex gap-1 bg-white rounded-lg p-1 shadow-sm w-fit\">\n        <button\n          onClick={() => setMode('land')}\n          className={`px-4 py-1.5 rounded text-sm font-medium ${mode === 'land' ? 'bg-[#5b3a1a] text-white' : 'text-gray-600 hover:bg-gray-100'}`}\n        >\n          Land (Umsetzer)\n        </button>\n        <button\n          onClick={() => setMode('bund')}\n          className={`px-4 py-1.5 rounded text-sm font-medium ${mode === 'bund' ? 'bg-[#5b3a1a] text-white' : 'text-gray-600 hover:bg-gray-100'}`}\n        >\n          Bund (Bundesland)\n        </button>\n      </div>\n\n      {/* Data entry area */}\n      {mode === 'land' ? (\n        <LandMode\n          exerciseId={parseInt(id!)}\n          repeaters={exercise.repeaters || []}\n          reports={reports}\n          onReportCreated={handleReportCreated}\n          onReportUpdated={handleReportUpdated}\n          onReportDeleted={handleReportDeleted}\n        />\n      ) : (\n        <BundMode\n          exerciseId={parseInt(id!)}\n          repeaters={exercise.repeaters || []}\n          reports={reports}\n          onReportCreated={handleReportCreated}\n          onReportUpdated={handleReportUpdated}\n          onReportDeleted={handleReportDeleted}\n        />\n      )}\n    </div>\n  );\n}\n"
}

{ ...prev, status: newStatus } : null);\n  };\n\n  if (loading) return <p className=\"text-gray-500 p-4\">Laden...</p>;\n  if (!exercise) return <p className=\"text-red-500 p-4\">Übung nicht gefunden</p>;\n\n  const statusColor = exercise.status === 'active' ? 'bg-green-500' : exercise.status === 'completed' ? 'bg-blue-500' : 'bg-gray-400';\n  const statusLabel = exercise.status === 'active' ? 'Aktiv' : exercise.status === 'completed' ? 'Abgeschlossen' : 'Geplant';\n\n  return (\n    <div className=\"space-y-4\">\n      <div className=\"flex items-center justify-between flex-wrap gap-2\">\n        <div className=\"flex items-center gap-4\">\n          <h1 className=\"text-xl font-bold text-[#5b3a1a]\">\n            Übung {new Date(exercise.date + 'T00:00:00').toLocaleDateString('de-AT', { day: '2-digit', month: '2-digit', year: 'numeric' })}\n          </h1>\n          <span className={`px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full text-xs text-white ${statusColor}`}>{statusLabel}</span>\n        </div>\n        <div className=\"flex items-center gap-2\">\n          {exercise.status === 'planned' && (\n            <button onClick={() => handleStatusChange('active')} className=\"bg-green-600 hover:bg-green-700 text-white px-3 py-1 rounded text-sm\">\n              Starten\n            </button>\n          )}\n          {exercise.status === 'active' && (\n            <button onClick={() => handleStatusChange('completed')} className=\"bg-blue-600 hover:bg-blue-700 text-white px-3 py-1 rounded text-sm\">\n              Abschließen\n            </button>\n          )}\n          <Link to={`/exercises/${id}/setup`} className=\"bg-gray-200 hover:bg-gray-300 text-gray-700 px-3 py-1 rounded text-sm\">\n            Einrichten\n          </Link>\n          <Link to={`/exercises/${id}/reports`} className=\"bg-amber-100 hover:bg-amber-200 text-amber-800 px-3 py-1 rounded text-sm\">\n            Auswertung\n          </Link>\n        </div>\n      </div>\n\n      <RunningTotals stats={stats} />\n\n      <div className=\"flex gap-1 bg-white rounded-lg p-1 shadow-sm w-fit\">\n        <button\n          onClick={() => setMode('land')}\n          className={`px-4 py-1.5 rounded text-sm font-medium ${mode === 'land' ? 'bg-[#5b3a1a] text-white' : 'text-gray-600 hover:bg-gray-100'}`}\n        >\n          Land (Umsetzer)\n        </button>\n        <button\n          onClick={() => setMode('bund')}\n          className={`px-4 py-1.5 rounded text-sm font-medium ${mode === 'bund' ? 'bg-[#5b3a1a] text-white' : 'text-gray-600 hover:bg-gray-100'}`}\n        >\n          Bund (Bundesland)\n        </button>\n      </div>\n\n      {mode === 'land' ? (\n        <LandMode\n          exerciseId={parseInt(id!)}\n          repeaters={exercise.repeaters || []}\n          reports={reports}\n          onReportCreated={handleReportCreated}\n          onReportUpdated={handleReportUpdated}\n          onReportDeleted={handleReportDeleted}\n        />\n      ) : (\n        <BundMode\n          exerciseId={parseInt(id!)}\n          repeaters={exercise.repeaters || []}\n          reports={reports}\n          onReportCreated={handleReportCreated}\n          onReportUpdated={handleReportUpdated}\n          onReportDeleted={handleReportDeleted}\n        />\n      )}\n    </div>\n  );\n}\n"
}

  "content": "interface Props {\n  stats: {\n    totalParticipants: number;\n    totalReports: number;\n    perRepeater: { short_name: string; count: number }[];\n  };\n}\n\nexport default function RunningTotals({ stats }: Props) {\n  return (\n    <div className=\"bg-white rounded-lg shadow-sm px-4 py-2 flex flex-wrap gap-4 text-sm sticky top-0 z-10 border-b\">\n      <div className=\"font-medium text-[#5b3a1a]\">\n        Anwesend: <span className=\"font-bold\">{stats.totalParticipants}</span>\n      </div>\n      <div className=\"font-medium text-[#5b3a1a]\">\n        Rapporte: <span className=\"font-bold\">{stats.totalReports}</span>\n      </div>\n      <div className=\"border-l border-gray-300 pl-4 flex flex-wrap gap-3\">\n        {stats.perRepeater.map(r => (\n          <div key={r.short_name} className=\"text-gray-600\">\n            {r.short_name}: <span className=\"font-semibold text-gray-800\">{r.count}</span>\n          </div>\n        ))}\n      </div>\n    </div>\n  );\n}\n"
}

{
  "file_path": "/home/achildrenmile/bosarsalog/client/src/components/CallsignInput.tsx",
  "content": "import { useState, useRef, useEffect } from 'react';\nimport { apiFetch } from '../services/api';\n\ninterface Props {\n  value: string;\n  onChange: (value: string) => void;\n  onSelect: (operator: any) => void;\n  autoFocus?: boolean;\n  className?: string;\n}\n\nexport default function CallsignInput({ value, onChange, onSelect, autoFocus, className }: Props) {\n  const [suggestions, setSuggestions] = useState<any[]>([]);\n  const [showSuggestions, setShowSuggestions] = useState(false);\n  const [selectedIdx, setSelectedIdx] = useState(-1);\n  const inputRef = useRef<HTMLInputElement>(null);\n  const debounceRef = useRef<ReturnType<typeof setTimeout>>();\n\n  useEffect(() => {\n    if (autoFocus && inputRef.current) inputRef.current.focus();\n  }, [autoFocus]);\n\n  const search = (q: string) => {\n    if (debounceRef.current) clearTimeout(debounceRef.current);\n    if (q.length < 2) { setSuggestions([]); setShowSuggestions(false); return; }\n    debounceRef.current = setTimeout(async () => {\n      try {\n        const results = await apiFetch(`/api/v1/operators?q=${encodeURIComponent(q)}`);\n        setSuggestions(results);\n        setShowSuggestions(results.length > 0);\n        setSelectedIdx(-1);\n      } catch {}\n    }, 150);\n  };\n\n  const handleChange = (val: string) => {\n    const upper = val.toUpperCase();\n    onChange(upper);\n    search(upper);\n  };\n\n  const handleKeyDown = (e: React.KeyboardEvent) => {\n    if (e.key === 'ArrowDown') {\n      e.preventDefault();\n      setSelectedIdx(prev => Math.min(prev + 1, suggestions.length - 1));\n    } else if (e.key === 'ArrowUp') {\n      e.preventDefault();\n      setSelectedIdx(prev => Math.max(prev - 1, -1));\n    } else if (e.key === 'Enter' && selectedIdx >= 0) {\n      e.preventDefault();\n      selectSuggestion(suggestions[selectedIdx]);\n    } else if (e.key === 'Escape') {\n      setShowSuggestions(false);\n    }\n    // Keyboard shortcuts\n    if (e.altKey && e.key >= '1' && e.key <= '9') {\n      e.preventDefault();\n      onChange(value + `5/${e.key}`);\n    }\n    if (e.ctrlKey && e.key >= '1' && e.key <= '9') {\n      e.preventDefault();\n      onChange(`OE${e.key}`);\n    }\n  };\n\n  const selectSuggestion = (op: any) => {\n    onChange(op.callsign);\n    onSelect(op);\n    setShowSuggestions(false);\n    setSuggestions([]);\n  };\n\n  return (\n    <div className=\"relative\">\n      <input\n        ref={inputRef}\n        type=\"text\"\n        value={value}\n        onChange={e => handleChange(e.target.value)}\n        onKeyDown={handleKeyDown}\n        onBlur={() => setTimeout(() => setShowSuggestions(false), 200)}\n        onFocus={() => { if (suggestions.length > 0) setShowSuggestions(true); }}\n        className={`border border-gray-300 rounded px-2 py-1 font-mono uppercase text-sm focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-amber-500 ${className || 'w-32'}`}\n        placeholder=\"Rufzeichen\"\n      />\n      {showSuggestions && (\n        <div className=\"absolute z-20 top-full left-0 mt-1 bg-white border rounded shadow-lg max-h-48 overflow-auto w-64\">\n          {suggestions.map((op, i) => (\n            <div\n              key={op.id}\n              onMouseDown={() => selectSuggestion(op)}\n              className={`px-3 py-1.5 cursor-pointer text-sm ${i === selectedIdx ? 'bg-amber-100' : 'hover:bg-gray-100'}`}\n            >\n              <span className=\"font-mono font-bold\">{op.callsign}</span>\n              {op.name && <span className=\"ml-2 text-gray-500\">{op.name}</span>}\n              {op.bezirk_code && <span className=\"ml-1 text-xs bg-gray-200 rounded px-1\">{op.bezirk_code}</span>}\n            </div>\n          ))}\n        </div>\n      )}\n    </div>\n  );\n}\n"
}

{
  "file_path": "/home/achildrenmile/bosarsalog/client/src/components/LandMode.tsx",
  "content": "import { useState, useEffect, useCallback, useRef, type FormEvent } from 'react';\nimport { apiFetch } from '../services/api';\nimport CallsignInput from './CallsignInput';\n\ninterface Props {\n  exerciseId: number;\n  repeaters: any[];\n  reports: any[];\n  onReportCreated: (report: any) => void;\n  onReportUpdated: (report: any) => void;\n  onReportDeleted: (reportId: number) => void;\n}\n\nexport default function LandMode({ exerciseId, repeaters, reports, onReportCreated, onReportUpdated, onReportDeleted }: Props) {\n  const [activeRepeaterId, setActiveRepeaterId] = useState<number | null>(null);\n  const [bezirke, setBezirke] = useState<any[]>([]);\n  const [allBezirke, setAllBezirke] = useState<any[]>([]);\n  const [bundeslaender, setBundeslaender] = useState<any[]>([]);\n\n  // Entry form state\n  const [callsign, setCallsign] = useState('');\n  const [selectedOperator, setSelectedOperator] = useState<any>(null);\n  const [rapport, setRapport] = useState('');\n  const [notes, setNotes] = useState('');\n  const [editingId, setEditingId] = useState<number | null>(null);\n  const callsignRef = useRef<HTMLInputElement>(null);\n\n  useEffect(() => {\n    apiFetch('/api/v1/reference/bundeslaender').then(setBundeslaender).catch(() => {});\n    apiFetch('/api/v1/reference/bezirke').then(data => {\n      setAllBezirke(data);\n      setBezirke(data);\n    }).catch(() => {});\n  }, []);\n\n  useEffect(() => {\n    if (repeaters.length > 0 && !activeRepeaterId) {\n      setActiveRepeaterId(repeaters[0].repeater_id);\n    }\n  }, [repeaters, activeRepeaterId]);\n\n  const activeRepeater = repeaters.find(r => r.repeater_id === activeRepeaterId);\n  const repeaterReports = reports.filter(r => r.repeater_id === activeRepeaterId);\n\n  const parseRapport = (raw: string) => {\n    const match = raw.match(/^(\\d)\\/(\\d)(.*)$/);\n    if (!match) return { readability: null, strength: null, db_over_s9: null };\n    return {\n      readability: parseInt(match[1]),\n      strength: parseInt(match[2]),\n      db_over_s9: match[3]?.trim() || null,\n    };\n  };\n\n  const handleSubmit = async (e: FormEvent) => {\n    e.preventDefault();\n    if (!selectedOperator || !activeRepeaterId) return;\n\n    const parsed = parseRapport(rapport);\n\n    if (editingId) {\n      try {\n        const updated = await apiFetch(`/api/v1/exercises/${exerciseId}/reports/${editingId}`, {\n          method: 'PATCH',\n          body: JSON.stringify({ ...parsed, notes: notes || null }),\n        });\n        onReportUpdated(updated);\n        setEditingId(null);\n      } catch {}\n    } else {\n      try {\n        const report = await apiFetch(`/api/v1/exercises/${exerciseId}/reports`, {\n          method: 'POST',\n          body: JSON.stringify({\n            operator_id: selectedOperator.id,\n            repeater_id: activeRepeaterId,\n            ...parsed,\n            notes: notes || null,\n          }),\n        });\n        onReportCreated(report);\n\n        // Cross-repeater sync: create placeholder entries on other repeaters\n        for (const rep of repeaters) {\n          if (rep.repeater_id !== activeRepeaterId) {\n            const exists = reports.some(r => r.operator_id === selectedOperator.id && r.repeater_id === rep.repeater_id);\n            if (!exists) {\n              try {\n                const placeholder = await apiFetch(`/api/v1/exercises/${exerciseId}/reports`, {\n                  method: 'POST',\n                  body: JSON.stringify({\n                    operator_id: selectedOperator.id,\n                    repeater_id: rep.repeater_id,\n                  }),\n                });\n                onReportCreated(placeholder);\n              } catch {}\n            }\n          }\n        }\n      } catch (err: any) {\n        if (err.message?.includes('existiert')) {\n          // Update existing\n          const existing = reports.find(r => r.operator_id === selectedOperator.id && r.repeater_id === activeRepeaterId);\n          if (existing) {\n            const updated = await apiFetch(`/api/v1/exercises/${exerciseId}/reports/${existing.id}`, {\n              method: 'PATCH',\n              body: JSON.stringify({ ...parsed, notes: notes || null }),\n            });\n            onReportUpdated(updated);\n          }\n        }\n      }\n    }\n\n    setCallsign('');\n    setSelectedOperator(null);\n    setRapport('');\n    setNotes('');\n  };\n\n  const handleEdit = (report: any) => {\n    setEditingId(report.id);\n    setCallsign(report.callsign);\n    setSelectedOperator({ id: report.operator_id, callsign: report.callsign });\n    const rapportStr = report.readability && report.strength\n      ? `${report.readability}/${report.strength}${report.db_over_s9 || ''}`\n      : '';\n    setRapport(rapportStr);\n    setNotes(report.notes || '');\n  };\n\n  const handleDelete = async (reportId: number) => {\n    if (!confirm('Rapport löschen?')) return;\n    try {\n      await apiFetch(`/api/v1/exercises/${exerciseId}/reports/${reportId}`, { method: 'DELETE' });\n      onReportDeleted(reportId);\n    } catch {}\n  };\n\n  // Group reports by Bezirk\n  const reportsByBezirk: Record<string, any[]> = {};\n  for (const r of repeaterReports) {\n    const key = r.bezirk_code || '_other';\n    if (!reportsByBezirk[key]) reportsByBezirk[key] = [];\n    reportsByBezirk[key].push(r);\n  }\n\n  return (\n    <div className=\"space-y-3\">\n      {/* Repeater tabs */}\n      <div className=\"flex flex-wrap gap-1\">\n        {repeaters.map(r => (\n          <button\n            key={r.repeater_id}\n            onClick={() => setActiveRepeaterId(r.repeater_id)}\n            className={`px-3 py-1.5 rounded text-sm ${r.repeater_id === activeRepeaterId ? 'bg-[#5b3a1a] text-white font-medium' : 'bg-white text-gray-700 hover:bg-gray-100 border'}`}\n          >\n            {r.short_name}\n            {repeaterReports.length > 0 && r.repeater_id === activeRepeaterId && (\n              <span className=\"ml-1 bg-white/20 rounded-full px-1.5 text-xs\">{repeaterReports.filter(rp => !rp.is_op_marker && rp.readability).length}</span>\n            )}\n          </button>\n        ))}\n      </div>\n\n      {/* Repeater info */}\n      {activeRepeater && (\n        <div className=\"text-xs text-gray-500 flex gap-4\">\n          {activeRepeater.frequency_mhz && <span>{activeRepeater.frequency_mhz} MHz</span>}\n          {activeRepeater.offset_mhz && <span>Offset: {activeRepeater.offset_mhz > 0 ? '+' : ''}{activeRepeater.offset_mhz} MHz</span>}\n          {activeRepeater.ctcss_hz && <span>CTCSS: {activeRepeater.ctcss_hz} Hz</span>}\n          {activeRepeater.burst_hz && <span>Burst: {activeRepeater.burst_hz} Hz</span>}\n          {activeRepeater.repeater_callsign && <span className=\"font-mono\">{activeRepeater.repeater_callsign}</span>}\n        </div>\n      )}\n\n      {/* Entry form */}\n      <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className=\"bg-white rounded-lg shadow-sm p-3 flex items-center gap-2 flex-wrap\">\n        <CallsignInput\n          value={callsign}\n          onChange={setCallsign}\n          onSelect={setSelectedOperator}\n          autoFocus\n          className=\"w-36\"\n        />\n        <input\n          type=\"text\"\n          value={rapport}\n          onChange={e => setRapport(e.target.value.toUpperCase())}\n          placeholder=\"5/9+20\"\n          className=\"border border-gray-300 rounded px-2 py-1 font-mono text-sm w-24 focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-amber-500\"\n          onKeyDown={e => {\n            if (e.altKey && e.key >= '1' && e.key <= '9') {\n              e.preventDefault();\n              setRapport(`5/${e.key}`);\n            }\n          }}\n        />\n        <input\n          type=\"text\"\n          value={notes}\n          onChange={e => setNotes(e.target.value)}\n          placeholder=\"Sonstiges\"\n          className=\"border border-gray-300 rounded px-2 py-1 text-sm flex-1 min-w-[100px] focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-amber-500\"\n        />\n        <button type=\"submit\" className=\"bg-[#5b3a1a] hover:bg-[#7a5230] text-white px-4 py-1 rounded text-sm font-medium\">\n          {editingId ? 'Speichern' : 'Eintragen'}\n        </button>\n        {editingId && (\n          <button type=\"button\" onClick={() => { setEditingId(null); setCallsign(''); setSelectedOperator(null); setRapport(''); setNotes(''); }} className=\"text-gray-500 text-sm hover:text-gray-700\">\n            Abbrechen\n          </button>\n        )}\n      </form>\n\n      {/* Reports grouped by Bezirk */}\n      <div className=\"space-y-2\">\n        {bundeslaender.filter(bl => !bl.is_international).map(bl => {\n          const blBezirke = allBezirke.filter(b => b.bundesland_code === bl.code);\n          const blReports = repeaterReports.filter(r => r.bundesland_code === bl.code);\n          if (blReports.length === 0) return null;\n\n          return (\n            <div key={bl.code} className=\"bg-white rounded-lg shadow-sm overflow-hidden\">\n              <div className=\"bg-gray-50 px-3 py-1.5 flex items-center gap-2 border-b\">\n                <span className=\"font-medium text-sm\">{bl.name}</span>\n                <span className=\"bg-[#0d6efd] text-white text-xs px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded-full\">{blReports.filter(r => !r.is_op_marker && r.readability).length}</span>\n              </div>\n              <div className=\"divide-y divide-gray-100\">\n                {blBezirke.map(bz => {\n                  const bzReports = blReports.filter(r => r.bezirk_code === bz.code);\n                  if (bzReports.length === 0) return null;\n                  return (\n                    <div key={bz.code} className=\"px-3 py-1 flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2 text-sm\">\n                      <span className={`text-xs font-mono px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded text-white ${bz.is_capital ? 'bg-[#dc3545]' : 'bg-[#6c757d]'}`}>\n                        {bz.code}\n                      </span>\n                      <div className=\"flex flex-wrap gap-2 flex-1\">\n                        {bzReports.map(r => (\n                          <span\n                            key={r.id}\n                            onClick={() => handleEdit(r)}\n                            className=\"cursor-pointer hover:bg-amber-50 px-1 rounded group\"\n                          >\n                            <span className=\"font-mono text-xs\">{r.callsign}</span>\n                            {r.readability && r.strength && (\n                              <span className=\"ml-1 text-gray-500 text-xs\">\n                                {r.readability}/{r.strength}{r.db_over_s9 || ''}\n                              </span>\n                            )}\n                            {r.notes && <span className=\"ml-1 text-amber-600 text-xs\">({r.notes})</span>}\n                            <button\n                              onClick={e => { e.stopPropagation(); handleDelete(r.id); }}\n                              className=\"ml-1 text-red-400 hover:text-red-600 opacity-0 group-hover:opacity-100 text-xs\"\n                            >\n                              x\n                            </button>\n                          </span>\n                        ))}\n                      </div>\n                    </div>\n                  );\n                })}\n              </div>\n            </div>\n          );\n        })}\n\n        {/* Uncategorized */}\n        {reportsByBezirk['_other']?.length > 0 && (\n          <div className=\"bg-white rounded-lg shadow-sm p-3\">\n            <div className=\"text-sm font-medium mb-1\">Sonstige</div>\n            {reportsByBezirk['_other'].map(r => (\n              <span key={r.id} onClick={() => handleEdit(r)} className=\"cursor-pointer hover:bg-amber-50 px-1 rounded text-sm\">\n                <span className=\"font-mono text-xs\">{r.callsign}</span>\n                {r.readability && r.strength && (\n                  <span className=\"ml-1 text-gray-500 text-xs\">{r.readability}/{r.strength}{r.db_over_s9 || ''}</span>\n                )}\n              </span>\n            ))}\n          </div>\n        )}\n      </div>\n    </div>\n  );\n}\n"
}
