```json
  "content": "import { useState, useEffect, type FormEvent } from 'react';\nimport { apiFetch } from '../services/api';\nimport CallsignInput from './CallsignInput';\n\ninterface Props {\n  exerciseId: number;\n  repeaters: any[];\n  reports: any[];\n  onReportCreated: (report: any) => void;\n  onReportUpdated: (report: any) => void;\n  onReportDeleted: (reportId: number) => void;\n}\n\ninterface EntryForm {\n  callsign: string;\n  operator: any;\n  rapport: string;\n  einstiegspunktId: number | null;\n  notes: string;\n}\n\nexport default function BundMode({ exerciseId, repeaters, reports, onReportCreated, onReportUpdated, onReportDeleted }: Props) {\n  const [bundeslaender, setBundeslaender] = useState<any[]>([]);\n  const [bezirke, setBezirke] = useState<any[]>([]);\n  const [einstiegspunkte, setEinstiegspunkte] = useState<any[]>([]);\n  const [collapsedBl, setCollapsedBl] = useState<Set<string>>(new Set());\n  const [blEpSelection, setBlEpSelection] = useState<Record<string, number | null>>({});\n  const [editingId, setEditingId] = useState<number | null>(null);\n  const [editForm, setEditForm] = useState<EntryForm>({ callsign: '', operator: null, rapport: '', einstiegspunktId: null, notes: '' });\n\n  // Linked repeater for Einstiegspunkt dropdown\n  const linkedRepeater = repeaters.find(r => r.is_linked);\n\n  useEffect(() => {\n    apiFetch('/api/v1/reference/bundeslaender').then(setBundeslaender).catch(() => {});\n    apiFetch('/api/v1/reference/bezirke').then(setBezirke).catch(() => {});\n  }, []);\n\n  useEffect(() => {\n    if (linkedRepeater) {\n      apiFetch(`/api/v1/repeaters/${linkedRepeater.repeater_id}/einstiegspunkte`)\n        .then(setEinstiegspunkte).catch(() => {});\n    }\n  }, [linkedRepeater]);\n\n  const toggleBl = (code: string) => {\n    setCollapsedBl(prev => {\n      const next = new Set(prev);\n      if (next.has(code)) next.delete(code); else next.add(code);\n      return next;\n    });\n  };\n\n  const parseRapport = (raw: string) => {\n    const match = raw.match(/^(\\d)\\/(\\d)(.*)$/);\n    if (!match) return { readability: null, strength: null, db_over_s9: null };\n    return {\n      readability: parseInt(match[1]),\n      strength: parseInt(match[2]),\n      db_over_s9: match[3]?.trim() || null,\n    };\n  };\n\n  const handleBezirkSubmit = async (bezirkCode: string, bundeslandCode: string, form: EntryForm) => {\n    if (!form.operator) return;\n    if (!linkedRepeater) return;\n\n    const parsed = parseRapport(form.rapport);\n    const epId = form.einstiegspunktId || blEpSelection[bundeslandCode] || null;\n\n    try {\n      const report = await apiFetch(`/api/v1/exercises/${exerciseId}/reports`, {\n        method: 'POST',\n        body: JSON.stringify({\n          operator_id: form.operator.id,\n          repeater_id: linkedRepeater.repeater_id,\n          ...parsed,\n          einstiegspunkt_id: epId,\n          notes: form.notes || null,\n        }),\n      });\n      onReportCreated(report);\n    } catch (err: any) {\n      if (err.message?.includes('existiert')) {\n        const existing = reports.find(r => r.operator_id === form.operator.id && r.repeater_id === linkedRepeater.repeater_id);\n        if (existing) {\n          const updated = await apiFetch(`/api/v1/exercises/${exerciseId}/reports/${existing.id}`, {\n            method: 'PATCH',\n            body: JSON.stringify({ ...parsed, einstiegspunkt_id: epId, notes: form.notes || null }),\n          });\n          onReportUpdated(updated);\n        }\n      }\n    }\n  };\n\n  const handleEpChange = (bundeslandCode: string, epId: number | null) => {\n    // Auto-propagate: set this EP for the entire Bundesland\n    setBlEpSelection(prev => ({ ...prev, [bundeslandCode]: epId }));\n  };\n\n  const handleEdit = (report: any) => {\n    setEditingId(report.id);\n    setEditForm({\n      callsign: report.callsign,\n      operator: { id: report.operator_id, callsign: report.callsign },\n      rapport: report.readability && report.strength ? `${report.readability}/${report.strength}${report.db_over_s9 || ''}` : '',\n      einstiegspunktId: report.einstiegspunkt_id || null,\n      notes: report.notes || '',\n    });\n  };\n\n  const handleUpdate = async () => {\n    if (!editingId) return;\n    const parsed = parseRapport(editForm.rapport);\n    try {\n      const updated = await apiFetch(`/api/v1/exercises/${exerciseId}/reports/${editingId}`, {\n        method: 'PATCH',\n        body: JSON.stringify({ ...parsed, einstiegspunkt_id: editForm.einstiegspunktId, notes: editForm.notes || null }),\n      });\n      onReportUpdated(updated);\n      setEditingId(null);\n    } catch {}\n  };\n\n  const handleDelete = async (reportId: number) => {\n    if (!confirm('Rapport löschen?')) return;\n    try {\n      await apiFetch(`/api/v1/exercises/${exerciseId}/reports/${reportId}`, { method: 'DELETE' });\n      onReportDeleted(reportId);\n    } catch {}\n  };\n\n  return (\n    <div className=\"space-y-2\">\n      {bundeslaender.filter(bl => !bl.is_international).map(bl => {\n        const blBezirke = bezirke.filter(b => b.bundesland_code === bl.code);\n        const blReports = reports.filter(r => r.bundesland_code === bl.code && linkedRepeater && r.repeater_id === linkedRepeater.repeater_id);\n        const isCollapsed = collapsedBl.has(bl.code);\n        const reportCount = blReports.filter(r => !r.is_op_marker && r.readability).length;\n\n        return (\n          <div key={bl.code} className=\"bg-white rounded-lg shadow-sm overflow-hidden\">\n            {/* Bundesland header */}\n            <div\n              onClick={() => toggleBl(bl.code)}\n              className=\"bg-gray-50 px-3 py-2 flex items-center justify-between cursor-pointer hover:bg-gray-100 border-b\"\n            >\n              <div className=\"flex items-center gap-2\">\n                <span className=\"text-xs text-gray-400\">{isCollapsed ? '▶' : '▼'}</span>\n                <span className=\"font-medium text-sm\">{bl.name}</span>\n                <span className=\"bg-[#0d6efd] text-white text-xs px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded-full\">{reportCount}</span>\n              </div>\n              {linkedRepeater && einstiegspunkte.length > 0 && (\n                <select\n                  value={blEpSelection[bl.code] || ''}\n                  onChange={e => {\n                    e.stopPropagation();\n                    handleEpChange(bl.code, e.target.value ? parseInt(e.target.value) : null);\n                  }}\n                  onClick={e => e.stopPropagation()}\n                  className=\"text-xs border border-gray-300 rounded px-1.5 py-0.5\"\n                >\n                  <option value=\"\">Einstiegspunkt</option>\n                  {einstiegspunkte.map(ep => (\n                    <option key={ep.id} value={ep.id}>{ep.abbreviation} ({ep.site_name})</option>\n                  ))}\n                </select>\n              )}\n            </div>\n\n            {/* Bezirk rows */}\n            {!isCollapsed && (\n              <div className=\"divide-y divide-gray-100\">\n                {blBezirke.map(bz => {\n                  const bzReports = blReports.filter(r => r.bezirk_code === bz.code);\n                  return (\n                    <BezirkRow\n                      key={bz.code}\n                      bezirk={bz}\n                      reports={bzReports}\n                      einstiegspunkte={einstiegspunkte}\n                      defaultEpId={blEpSelection[bl.code] || null}\n                      onSubmit={(form) => handleBezirkSubmit(bz.code, bl.code, form)}\n                      onEdit={handleEdit}\n                      onDelete={handleDelete}\n                      editingId={editingId}\n                      editForm={editForm}\n                      onEditChange={setEditForm}\n                      onEditSave={handleUpdate}\n                      onEditCancel={() => setEditingId(null)}\n                    />\n                  );\n                })}\n              </div>\n            )}\n          </div>\n        );\n      })}\n    </div>\n  );\n}\n\ninterface BezirkRowProps {\n  bezirk: any;\n  reports: any[];\n  einstiegspunkte: any[];\n  defaultEpId: number | null;\n  onSubmit: (form: EntryForm) => void;\n  onEdit: (report: any) => void;\n  onDelete: (id: number) => void;\n  editingId: number | null;\n  editForm: EntryForm;\n  onEditChange: (form: EntryForm) => void;\n  onEditSave: () => void;\n  onEditCancel: () => void;\n}\n\nfunction BezirkRow({ bezirk, reports, einstiegspunkte, defaultEpId, onSubmit, onEdit, onDelete, editingId, editForm, onEditChange, onEditSave, onEditCancel }: BezirkRowProps) {\n  const [form, setForm] = useState<EntryForm>({ callsign: '', operator: null, rapport: '', einstiegspunktId: null, notes: '' });\n\n  const handleSubmit = (e: FormEvent) => {\n    e.preventDefault();\n    if (!form.operator) return;\n    onSubmit({ ...form, einstiegspunktId: form.einstiegspunktId || defaultEpId });\n    setForm({ callsign: '', operator: null, rapport: '', einstiegspunktId: null, notes: '' });\n  };\n\n  return (\n    <div className=\"px-3 py-1.5\">\n      <div className=\"flex items-center gap-2 mb-1\">\n        <span className={`text-xs font-mono px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded text-white ${bezirk.is_capital ? 'bg-[#dc3545]' : 'bg-[#6c757d]'}`}>\n          {bezirk.code}\n        </span>\n        <span className=\"text-xs text-gray-500\">{bezirk.name}</span>\n      </div>\n\n      {/* Existing reports */}\n      <div className=\"flex flex-wrap gap-1 mb-1\">\n        {reports.map(r => (\n          <div key={r.id} className=\"group\">\n            {editingId === r.id ? (\n              <div className=\"flex items-center gap-1 bg-amber-50 rounded p-1\">\n                <input value={editForm.rapport} onChange={e => onEditChange({ ...editForm, rapport: e.target.value.toUpperCase() })} className=\"border rounded px-1 py-0.5 font-mono text-xs w-20\" />\n                <button onClick={onEditSave} className=\"text-green-600 text-xs font-bold\">OK</button>\n                <button onClick={onEditCancel} className=\"text-gray-400 text-xs\">X</button>\n              </div>\n            ) : (\n              <span\n                onClick={() => onEdit(r)}\n                className=\"inline-flex items-center gap-1 bg-gray-50 hover:bg-amber-50 rounded px-1.5 py-0.5 cursor-pointer text-xs\"\n              >\n                <span className=\"font-mono font-medium\">{r.callsign}</span>\n                {r.readability && r.strength && (\n                  <span className=\"text-gray-500\">{r.readability}/{r.strength}{r.db_over_s9 || ''}</span>\n                )}\n                {r.einstiegspunkt_abbr && <span className=\"text-blue-600\">{r.einstiegspunkt_abbr}</span>}\n                {r.notes && <span className=\"text-amber-600\">({r.notes})</span>}\n                <button\n                  onClick={e => { e.stopPropagation(); onDelete(r.id); }}\n                  className=\"text-red-400 hover:text-red-600 opacity-0 group-hover:opacity-100\"\n                >\n                  x\n                </button>\n              </span>\n            )}\n          </div>\n        ))}\n      </div>\n\n      {/* Inline entry form */}\n      <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className=\"flex items-center gap-1\">\n        <CallsignInput\n          value={form.callsign}\n          onChange={v => setForm(f => ({ ...f, callsign: v }))}\n          onSelect={op => setForm(f => ({ ...f, operator: op, callsign: op.callsign }))}\n          className=\"w-28\"\n        />\n        <input\n          type=\"text\"\n          value={form.rapport}\n          onChange={e => setForm(f => ({ ...f, rapport: e.target.value.toUpperCase() }))}\n          placeholder=\"5/9\"\n          className=\"border border-gray-300 rounded px-1.5 py-0.5 font-mono text-xs w-20 focus:outline-none focus:ring-1 focus:ring-amber-500\"\n          onKeyDown={e => {\n            if (e.altKey && e.key >= '1' && e.key <= '9') {\n              e.preventDefault();\n              setForm(f => ({ ...f, rapport: `5/${e.key}` }));\n            }\n          }}\n        />\n        {einstiegspunkte.length > 0 && (\n          <select\n            value={form.einstiegspunktId || defaultEpId || ''}\n            onChange={e => setForm(f => ({ ...f, einstiegspunktId: e.target.value ? parseInt(e.target.value) : null }))}\n            className=\"text-xs border border-gray-300 rounded px-1 py-0.5\"\n          >\n            <option value=\"\">EP</option>\n            {einstiegspunkte.map(ep => (\n              <option key={ep.id} value={ep.id}>{ep.abbreviation}</option>\n            ))}\n          </select>\n        )}\n        <input\n          type=\"text\"\n          value={form.notes}\n          onChange={e => setForm(f => ({ ...f, notes: e.target.value }))}\n          placeholder=\"Sonst.\"\n          className=\"border border-gray-300 rounded px-1.5 py-0.5 text-xs flex-1 min-w-[60px] focus:outline-none focus:ring-1 focus:ring-amber-500\"\n        />\n        <button type=\"submit\" className=\"bg-[#5b3a1a] hover:bg-[#7a5230] text-white px-2 py-0.5 rounded text-xs\">\n          +\n        </button>\n      </form>\n    </div>\n  );\n}\n"
}
```

```json
  "content": "import { useState, useEffect } from 'react';\nimport { useParams, Link, useNavigate } from 'react-router-dom';\nimport { apiFetch } from '../services/api';\n\nexport default function ExerciseSetupPage() {\n  const { id } = useParams<{ id: string }>();\n  const navigate = useNavigate();\n  const [exercise, setExercise] = useState<any>(null);\n  const [allRepeaters, setAllRepeaters] = useState<any[]>([]);\n  const [activeRepeaters, setActiveRepeaters] = useState<any[]>([]);\n  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);\n\n  useEffect(() => {\n    if (!id) return;\n    Promise.all([\n      apiFetch(`/api/v1/exercises/${id}`),\n      apiFetch('/api/v1/repeaters'),\n    ]).then(([ex, reps]) => {\n      setExercise(ex);\n      setAllRepeaters(reps);\n      setActiveRepeaters(ex.repeaters || []);\n    }).catch(() => {}).finally(() => setLoading(false));\n  }, [id]);\n\n  const toggleRepeater = async (repeaterId: number) => {\n    const isActive = activeRepeaters.some(r => r.repeater_id === repeaterId);\n    if (isActive) {\n      await apiFetch(`/api/v1/exercises/${id}/repeaters/${repeaterId}`, { method: 'DELETE' });\n      setActiveRepeaters(prev => prev.filter(r => r.repeater_id !== repeaterId));\n    } else {\n      await apiFetch(`/api/v1/exercises/${id}/repeaters`, {\n        method: 'POST',\n        body: JSON.stringify({ repeater_id: repeaterId }),\n      });\n      const updated = await apiFetch(`/api/v1/exercises/${id}/repeaters`);\n      setActiveRepeaters(updated);\n    }\n  };\n\n  const updateOp = async (repeaterId: number, callsign: string) => {\n    await apiFetch(`/api/v1/exercises/${id}/repeaters/${repeaterId}`, {\n      method: 'PATCH',\n      body: JSON.stringify({ operator_callsign: callsign || null }),\n    });\n  };\n\n  const activateExercise = async () => {\n    await apiFetch(`/api/v1/exercises/${id}`, {\n      method: 'PATCH',\n      body: JSON.stringify({ status: 'active' }),\n    });\n    navigate(`/exercises/${id}`);\n  };\n\n  if (loading) return <p className=\"text-gray-500\">Laden...</p>;\n  if (!exercise) return <p className=\"text-red-500\">Übung nicht gefunden</p>;\n\n  return (\n    <div className=\"space-y-6\">\n      <div className=\"flex items-center justify-between\">\n        <h1 className=\"text-xl font-bold text-[#5b3a1a]\">\n          Übung einrichten — {new Date(exercise.date + 'T00:00:00').toLocaleDateString('de-AT')}\n        </h1>\n        <div className=\"flex gap-2\">\n          <Link to={`/exercises/${id}`} className=\"bg-gray-200 hover:bg-gray-300 text-gray-700 px-3 py-1.5 rounded text-sm\">\n            Zurück\n          </Link>\n          <button onClick={activateExercise} className=\"bg-green-600 hover:bg-green-700 text-white px-4 py-1.5 rounded text-sm font-medium\">\n            Übung starten\n          </button>\n        </div>\n      </div>\n\n      <div className=\"bg-white rounded-xl shadow p-4\">\n        <h2 className=\"text-lg font-semibold text-[#5b3a1a] mb-3\">Umsetzer aktivieren</h2>\n        <div className=\"space-y-2\">\n          {allRepeaters.map(rep => {\n            const active = activeRepeaters.find(r => r.repeater_id === rep.id);\n            return (\n              <div key={rep.id} className=\"flex items-center gap-3 py-2 border-b border-gray-100\">\n                <label className=\"flex items-center gap-2 cursor-pointer flex-1\">\n                  <input\n                    type=\"checkbox\"\n                    checked={!!active}\n                    onChange={() => toggleRepeater(rep.id)}\n                    className=\"w-4 h-4 accent-amber-600\"\n                  />\n                  <span className=\"font-medium text-sm\">{rep.short_name}</span>\n                  <span className=\"text-xs text-gray-500\">\n                    {rep.frequency_mhz && `${rep.frequency_mhz} MHz`}\n                    {rep.callsign && ` (${rep.callsign})`}\n                  </span>\n                  {rep.is_linked ? <span className=\"text-xs bg-blue-100 text-blue-700 px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded\">linked</span> : null}\n                </label>\n                {active && (\n                  <input\n                    type=\"text\"\n                    placeholder=\"OP Rufzeichen\"\n                    defaultValue={active.operator_callsign || ''}\n                    onBlur={e => updateOp(rep.id, e.target.value.toUpperCase())}\n                    className=\"border border-gray-300 rounded px-2 py-1 text-sm font-mono uppercase w-28\"\n                  />\n                )}\n              </div>\n            );\n          })}\n        </div>\n      </div>\n    </div>\n  );\n}\n"
}
```

```json
  "content": "import { useState, useEffect } from 'react';\nimport { useParams, Link } from 'react-router-dom';\nimport { apiFetch } from '../services/api';\n\nexport default function ReportsPage() {\n  const { id } = useParams<{ id: string }>();\n  const [exercise, setExercise] = useState<any>(null);\n  const [stats, setStats] = useState<any>(null);\n  const [nebenstationen, setNebenstationen] = useState<any[]>([]);\n  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);\n\n  useEffect(() => {\n    if (!id) return;\n    Promise.all([\n      apiFetch(`/api/v1/exercises/${id}`),\n      apiFetch(`/api/v1/exercises/${id}/stats`),\n      apiFetch(`/api/v1/exercises/${id}/nebenstationen`),\n    ]).then(([ex, st, nb]) => {\n      setExercise(ex);\n      setStats(st);\n      setNebenstationen(nb);\n    }).catch(() => {}).finally(() => setLoading(false));\n  }, [id]);\n\n  if (loading) return <p className=\"text-gray-500\">Laden...</p>;\n  if (!exercise) return <p className=\"text-red-500\">Nicht gefunden</p>;\n\n  // Group nebenstationen by Bundesland\n  const byBl: Record<string, any[]> = {};\n  for (const n of nebenstationen) {\n    if (!byBl[n.bundesland]) byBl[n.bundesland] = [];\n    byBl[n.bundesland].push(n);\n  }\n\n  return (\n    <div className=\"space-y-6\">\n      <div className=\"flex items-center justify-between\">\n        <h1 className=\"text-xl font-bold text-[#5b3a1a]\">\n          Auswertung — {new Date(exercise.date + 'T00:00:00').toLocaleDateString('de-AT')}\n        </h1>\n        <Link to={`/exercises/${id}`} className=\"bg-gray-200 hover:bg-gray-300 text-gray-700 px-3 py-1.5 rounded text-sm\">\n          Zurück\n        </Link>\n      </div>\n\n      {/* Summary stats */}\n      {stats && (\n        <div className=\"grid grid-cols-2 md:grid-cols-4 gap-3\">\n          <div className=\"bg-white rounded-lg shadow-sm p-4 text-center\">\n            <div className=\"text-3xl font-bold text-[#5b3a1a]\">{stats.totalParticipants}</div>\n            <div className=\"text-sm text-gray-500\">Teilnehmer</div>\n          </div>\n          <div className=\"bg-white rounded-lg shadow-sm p-4 text-center\">\n            <div className=\"text-3xl font-bold text-[#5b3a1a]\">{stats.totalReports}</div>\n            <div className=\"text-sm text-gray-500\">Rapporte</div>\n          </div>\n          {stats.perRepeater?.map((r: any) => (\n            <div key={r.short_name} className=\"bg-white rounded-lg shadow-sm p-4 text-center\">\n              <div className=\"text-2xl font-bold text-gray-700\">{r.count}</div>\n              <div className=\"text-xs text-gray-500\">{r.short_name}</div>\n            </div>\n          ))}\n        </div>\n      )}\n\n      {/* Nebenstationen */}\n      <div className=\"bg-white rounded-xl shadow p-4\">\n        <h2 className=\"text-lg font-semibold text-[#5b3a1a] mb-3\">Nebenstationen nach Bezirk</h2>\n        {Object.entries(byBl).map(([bl, districts]) => (\n          <div key={bl} className=\"mb-3\">\n            <h3 className=\"font-medium text-sm text-gray-700 mb-1\">{bl}</h3>\n            <div className=\"flex flex-wrap gap-2\">\n              {districts.map(d => (\n                <span\n                  key={d.bezirk_code}\n                  className={`text-xs px-2 py-1 rounded ${d.is_capital ? 'bg-red-100 text-red-800' : 'bg-gray-100 text-gray-700'}`}\n                >\n                  {d.bezirk_code}: {d.count}\n                </span>\n              ))}\n            </div>\n          </div>\n        ))}\n      </div>\n\n      {/* Export buttons */}\n      <div className=\"bg-white rounded-xl shadow p-4\">\n        <h2 className=\"text-lg font-semibold text-[#5b3a1a] mb-3\">Export</h2>\n        <div className=\"flex flex-wrap gap-3\">\n          <a\n            href={`/api/v1/export/exercises/${id}/bund`}\n            className=\"bg-amber-100 hover:bg-amber-200 text-amber-800 px-4 py-2 rounded text-sm font-medium\"\n            target=\"_blank\"\n          >\n            TXT — Bund (Bundesland)\n          </a>\n          <a\n            href={`/api/v1/export/exercises/${id}/land`}\n            className=\"bg-amber-100 hover:bg-amber-200 text-amber-800 px-4 py-2 rounded text-sm font-medium\"\n            target=\"_blank\"\n          >\n            TXT — Land (Umsetzer)\n          </a>\n          <a\n            href={`/api/v1/export/exercises/${id}/combined`}\n            className=\"bg-amber-100 hover:bg-amber-200 text-amber-800 px-4 py-2 rounded text-sm font-medium\"\n            target=\"_blank\"\n          >\n            TXT — Kombiniert\n          </a>\n        </div>\n      </div>\n    </div>\n  );\n}\n"
}
```

```json
  "content": "import { useState, useEffect, useRef } from 'react';\nimport { apiFetch } from '../services/api';\n\nexport default function OperatorsPage() {\n  const [operators, setOperators] = useState<any[]>([]);\n  const [total, setTotal] = useState(0);\n  const [search, setSearch] = useState('');\n  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);\n  const [editing, setEditing] = useState<any>(null);\n  const [showAdd, setShowAdd] = useState(false);\n  const [newOp, setNewOp] = useState({ callsign: '', name: '', qth: '', bezirk_code: '', bundesland_code: '' });\n  const [page, setPage] = useState(0);\n  const debounceRef = useRef<ReturnType<typeof setTimeout>>();\n\n  const load = async (q?: string, offset = 0) => {\n    setLoading(true);\n    try {\n      if (q) {\n        const results = await apiFetch(`/api/v1/operators?q=${encodeURIComponent(q)}`);\n        setOperators(results);\n        setTotal(results.length);\n      } else {\n        const data = await apiFetch(`/api/v1/operators?limit=50&offset=${offset}`);\n        setOperators(data.operators);\n        setTotal(data.total);\n      }\n    } catch {} finally { setLoading(false); }\n  };\n\n  useEffect(() => { load(); }, []);\n\n  const handleSearch = (q: string) => {\n    setSearch(q);\n    if (debounceRef.current) clearTimeout(debounceRef.current);\n    debounceRef.current = setTimeout(() => load(q), 300);\n  };\n\n  const handleSave = async (op: any) => {\n    try {\n      const updated = await apiFetch(`/api/v1/operators/${op.id}`, {\n        method: 'PATCH',\n        body: JSON.stringify(op),\n      });\n      setOperators(prev => prev.map(o => o.id === updated.id ? updated : o));\n      setEditing(null);\n    } catch {}\n  };\n\n  const handleAdd = async () => {\n    try {\n      const created = await apiFetch('/api/v1/operators', {\n        method: 'POST',\n        body: JSON.stringify(newOp),\n      });\n      setOperators(prev => [created, ...prev]);\n      setShowAdd(false);\n      setNewOp({ callsign: '', name: '', qth: '', bezirk_code: '', bundesland_code: '' });\n    } catch (e: any) {\n      alert(e.message);\n    }\n  };\n\n  const handlePage = (dir: number) => {\n    const newPage = page + dir;\n    setPage(newPage);\n    load(undefined, newPage * 50);\n  };\n\n  return (\n    <div className=\"space-y-4\">\n      <div className=\"flex items-center justify-between\">\n        <h1 className=\"text-xl font-bold text-[#5b3a1a]\">Rufzeichen-Register</h1>\n        <button onClick={() => setShowAdd(true)} className=\"bg-[#5b3a1a] hover:bg-[#7a5230] text-white px-3 py-1.5 rounded text-sm\">\n          + Neues Rufzeichen\n        </button>\n      </div>\n\n      <div className=\"flex items-center gap-3\">\n        <input\n          type=\"text\"\n          value={search}\n          onChange={e => handleSearch(e.target.value)}\n          placeholder=\"Rufzeichen, Name oder QTH suchen...\"\n          className=\"flex-1 border border-gray-300 rounded-lg px-3 py-2 text-sm focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-amber-500\"\n          autoFocus\n        />\n        <span className=\"text-sm text-gray-500\">{total} Einträge</span>\n      </div>\n\n      {showAdd && (\n        <div className=\"bg-white rounded-lg shadow p-4 space-y-2\">\n          <h3 className=\"font-medium text-sm\">Neues Rufzeichen anlegen</h3>\n          <div className=\"flex gap-2 flex-wrap\">\n            <input value={newOp.callsign} onChange={e => setNewOp(p => ({ ...p, callsign: e.target.value.toUpperCase() }))} placeholder=\"Rufzeichen\" className=\"border rounded px-2 py-1 text-sm font-mono uppercase w-28\" />\n            <input value={newOp.name} onChange={e => setNewOp(p => ({ ...p, name: e.target.value }))} placeholder=\"Name\" className=\"border rounded px-2 py-1 text-sm w-32\" />\n            <input value={newOp.qth} onChange={e => setNewOp(p => ({ ...p, qth: e.target.value }))} placeholder=\"QTH\" className=\"border rounded px-2 py-1 text-sm w-32\" />\n            <input value={newOp.bezirk_code} onChange={e => setNewOp(p => ({ ...p, bezirk_code: e.target.value.toUpperCase() }))} placeholder=\"Bezirk\" className=\"border rounded px-2 py-1 text-sm w-16 uppercase\" />\n            <input value={newOp.bundesland_code} onChange={e => setNewOp(p => ({ ...p, bundesland_code: e.target.value }))} placeholder=\"BL\" className=\"border rounded px-2 py-1 text-sm w-12\" />\n            <button onClick={handleAdd} className=\"bg-green-600 text-white px-3 py-1 rounded text-sm\">Anlegen</button>\n            <button onClick={() => setShowAdd(false)} className=\"text-gray-500 text-sm\">Abbrechen</button>\n          </div>\n        </div>\n      )}\n\n      <div className=\"bg-white rounded-xl shadow overflow-hidden\">\n        <table className=\"w-full text-sm\">\n          <thead className=\"bg-[#5b3a1a] text-white\">\n            <tr>\n              <th className=\"px-3 py-2 text-left\">Rufzeichen</th>\n              <th className=\"px-3 py-2 text-left\">Name</th>\n              <th className=\"px-3 py-2 text-left\">QTH</th>\n              <th className=\"px-3 py-2 text-left\">Bezirk</th>\n              <th className=\"px-3 py-2 text-left\">BL</th>\n              <th className=\"px-3 py-2 text-left\">Ausstattung</th>\n              <th className=\"px-3 py-2\"></th>\n            </tr>\n          </thead>\n          <tbody>\n            {operators.map(op => (\n              <tr key={op.id} className=\"border-t hover:bg-amber-50\">\n                {editing?.id === op.id ? (\n                  <>\n                    <td className=\"px-3 py-1\"><input value={editing.callsign} onChange={e => setEditing({ ...editing, callsign: e.target.value.toUpperCase() })} className=\"border rounded px-1 py-0.5 font-mono text-xs w-24 uppercase\" /></td>\n                    <td className=\"px-3 py-1\"><input value={editing.name || ''} onChange={e => setEditing({ ...editing, name: e.target.value })} className=\"border rounded px-1 py-0.5 text-xs w-24\" /></td>\n                    <td className=\"px-3 py-1\"><input value={editing.qth || ''} onChange={e => setEditing({ ...editing, qth: e.target.value })} className=\"border rounded px-1 py-0.5 text-xs w-24\" /></td>\n                    <td className=\"px-3 py-1\"><input value={editing.bezirk_code || ''} onChange={e => setEditing({ ...editing, bezirk_code: e.target.value.toUpperCase() })} className=\"border rounded px-1 py-0.5 text-xs w-12 uppercase\" /></td>\n                    <td className=\"px-3 py-1\"><input value={editing.bundesland_code || ''} onChange={e => setEditing({ ...editing, bundesland_code: e.target.value })} className=\"border rounded px-1 py-0.5 text-xs w-10\" /></td>\n                    <td className=\"px-3 py-1\"><input value={editing.equipment || ''} onChange={e => setEditing({ ...editing, equipment: e.target.value })} className=\"border rounded px-1 py-0.5 text-xs w-24\" /></td>\n                    <td className=\"px-3 py-1 flex gap-1\">\n                      <button onClick={() => handleSave(editing)} className=\"text-green-600 text-xs font-bold\">OK</button>\n                      <button onClick={() => setEditing(null)} className=\"text-gray-400 text-xs\">X</button>\n                    </td>\n                  </>\n                ) : (\n                  <>\n                    <td className=\"px-3 py-1.5 font-mono font-medium\">{op.callsign}</td>\n                    <td className=\"px-3 py-1.5\">{op.name}</td>\n                    <td className=\"px-3 py-1.5 text-gray-500\">{op.qth}</td>\n                    <td className=\"px-3 py-1.5\">\n                      {op.bezirk_code && (\n                        <span className=\"text-xs bg-gray-200 rounded px-1\">{op.bezirk_code}</span>\n                      )}\n                    </td>\n                    <td className=\"px-3 py-1.5 text-gray-500\">{op.bundesland_code}</td>\n                    <td className=\"px-3 py-1.5 text-xs text-gray-400\">{op.equipment}</td>\n                    <td className=\"px-3 py-1.5\">\n                      <button onClick={() => setEditing({ ...op })} className=\"text-amber-700 hover:underline text-xs\">\n                        Bearbeiten\n                      </button>\n                    </td>\n                  </>\n                )}\n              </tr>\n            ))}\n          </tbody>\n        </table>\n      </div>\n\n      {!search && (\n        <div className=\"flex items-center justify-center gap-4\">\n          <button onClick={() => handlePage(-1)} disabled={page === 0} className=\"text-sm text-gray-500 hover:text-gray-800 disabled:opacity-30\">\n            Zurück\n          </button>\n          <span className=\"text-sm text-gray-400\">Seite {page + 1}</span>\n          <button onClick={() => handlePage(1)} disabled={operators.length < 50} className=\"text-sm text-gray-500 hover:text-gray-800 disabled:opacity-30\">\n            Weiter\n          </button>\n        </div>\n      )}\n    </div>\n  );\n}\n"
}
```

```
     1→#!/bin/bash
     2→set -e
     3→
     4→# Colors for output
     5→RED='\033[0;31m'
     6→GREEN='\033[0;32m'
     7→YELLOW='\033[1;33m'
     8→NC='\033[0m' # No Color
     9→
    10→# Load environment variables
    11→if [ -f ".env.production" ]; then
    12→    source .env.production
    13→else
    14→    echo -e "${RED}Error: .env.production file not found${NC}"
    15→    echo "Copy .env.production.example to .env.production and configure it"
    16→    exit 1
    17→fi
    18→
    19→# Validate required variables
    20→REQUIRED_VARS="SYNOLOGY_HOST REMOTE_DIR CONTAINER_NAME IMAGE_NAME CONTAINER_PORT SITE_URL"
    21→for var in $REQUIRED_VARS; do
    22→    if [ -z "${!var}" ]; then
    23→        echo -e "${RED}Error: $var is not set in .env.production${NC}"
    24→        exit 1
    25→    fi
    26→done
    27→
    28→# Docker command (Synology uses a specific path)
    29→DOCKER_CMD="${DOCKER_CMD:-/var/packages/Docker/target/usr/bin/docker}"
    30→
    31→# Check for --rebuild flag
    32→REBUILD_FLAG=""
    33→if [ "$1" == "--rebuild" ]; then
    34→    REBUILD_FLAG="--no-cache"
    35→    echo -e "${YELLOW}Rebuild flag set - will build without cache${NC}"
    36→fi
    37→
    38→echo -e "${GREEN}=== Deploying BandBlick to Synology ===${NC}"
    39→echo "Host: $SYNOLOGY_HOST"
    40→echo "Remote dir: $REMOTE_DIR"
    41→echo "Container: $CONTAINER_NAME"
    42→echo "Port: $CONTAINER_PORT"
    43→echo ""
    44→
    45→# Step 1: Sync code to Synology
    46→echo -e "${GREEN}Step 1: Syncing code to Synology...${NC}"
    47→ssh "$SYNOLOGY_HOST" "
    48→    if [ -d '$REMOTE_DIR' ]; then
    49→        cd '$REMOTE_DIR' && git pull
    50→    else
    51→        git clone $(git remote get-url origin) '$REMOTE_DIR'
    52→    fi
    53→"
    54→
    55→# Step 2: Build Docker image on Synology
    56→echo -e "${GREEN}Step 2: Building Docker image on Synology...${NC}"
    57→ssh "$SYNOLOGY_HOST" "
    58→    cd '$REMOTE_DIR'
    59→    sudo $DOCKER_CMD build $REBUILD_FLAG -t '$IMAGE_NAME' .
    60→"
    61→
    62→# Step 3: Restart container
    63→echo -e "${GREEN}Step 3: Restarting container...${NC}"
    64→ssh "$SYNOLOGY_HOST" "
    65→    # Stop and remove existing container if it exists
    66→    sudo $DOCKER_CMD stop '$CONTAINER_NAME' 2>/dev/null || true
    67→    sudo $DOCKER_CMD rm '$CONTAINER_NAME' 2>/dev/null || true
    68→
    69→    # Start new container with health check
    70→    sudo $DOCKER_CMD run -d \
    71→        --name '$CONTAINER_NAME' \
    72→        --restart unless-stopped \
    73→        -p '$CONTAINER_PORT' \
    74→        -e SITE_NAME='BandBlick' \
    75→        -e SITE_DESCRIPTION='IARU Region 1 Band Plan Explorer' \
    76→        -e PARENT_SITE_NAME='OERadio' \
    77→        -e PARENT_SITE_URL='https://oeradio.at' \
    78→        -e PARENT_SITE_LOGO='https://oeradio.at/wp-content/uploads/2026/01/oeradiologo-300x200.png' \
    79→        --health-cmd='wget --no-verbose --tries=1 --spider http://localhost:80/ || exit 1' \
    80→        --health-interval=30s \
    81→        --health-timeout=10s \
    82→        --health-retries=3 \
    83→        --health-start-period=10s \
    84→        '$IMAGE_NAME'
    85→"
    86→
    87→# Step 4: Verify deployment
    88→echo -e "${GREEN}Step 4: Verifying deployment...${NC}"
    89→sleep 3
    90→
    91→# Check container status
    92→CONTAINER_STATUS=$(ssh "$SYNOLOGY_HOST" "sudo $DOCKER_CMD ps --filter 'name=$CONTAINER_NAME' --format '{{.Status}}'")
    93→echo "Container status: $CONTAINER_STATUS"
    94→
    95→# Check if site is accessible
    96→echo "Checking site accessibility..."
    97→LOCAL_PORT=$(echo $CONTAINER_PORT | cut -d: -f1)
    98→if ssh "$SYNOLOGY_HOST" "curl -s -o /dev/null -w '%{http_code}' http://localhost:$LOCAL_PORT" | grep -q "200"; then
    99→    echo -e "${GREEN}✓ Container is running on port $LOCAL_PORT${NC}"
   100→else
   101→    echo -e "${RED}✗ Container may not be accessible on port $LOCAL_PORT${NC}"
   102→fi
   103→
   104→# Check public URL (may fail if cloudflared not yet configured)
   105→if curl -s -o /dev/null -w "%{http_code}" "$SITE_URL" 2>/dev/null | grep -q "200"; then
   106→    echo -e "${GREEN}✓ Site is accessible at $SITE_URL${NC}"
   107→else
   108→    echo -e "${YELLOW}⚠ Public URL not yet accessible - configure cloudflared${NC}"
   109→fi
   110→
   111→echo ""
   112→echo -e "${GREEN}=== Deployment complete ===${NC}"
   113→
```

```
services:
  # CRISP - .NET 10 API + React frontend (single container)
  crisp:
    build:
      context: .
      dockerfile: src/CRISP.Api/Dockerfile
    container_name: crisp
    ports:
      - "5000:5000"
    environment:
      - ASPNETCORE_ENVIRONMENT=Production
      - ASPNETCORE_URLS=http://+:5000
      # Authentication
      - Auth__Enabled=${AUTH_ENABLED:-true}
      - Auth__Jwt__Secret=${JWT_SECRET}
      - Auth__Jwt__Issuer=${JWT_ISSUER:-CRISP}
      - Auth__Jwt__Audience=${JWT_AUDIENCE:-CRISP-Web}
      - Auth__Jwt__ExpirationMinutes=${JWT_EXPIRATION:-60}
      - Auth__ApiKeys__0__Id=default
      - Auth__ApiKeys__0__Name=${API_KEY_NAME:-default}
      - Auth__ApiKeys__0__Key=${API_KEY}
      - Auth__ApiKeys__0__IsHashed=false
      - Auth__ApiKeys__0__Roles__0=User
      - Auth__ApiKeys__0__Roles__1=Admin
      # Claude API
      - Claude__ApiKey=${CLAUDE_API_KEY}
      - Claude__Model=${CLAUDE_MODEL:-claude-sonnet-4-20250514}
      # Source Control
      - Crisp__ScmPlatform=${SCM_PLATFORM:-GitHub}
      - Crisp__GitHub__Owner=${GITHUB_OWNER}
      - Crisp__GitHub__Token=${GITHUB_TOKEN}
      - Crisp__GitHub__Visibility=${GITHUB_VISIBILITY:-Private}
      - Crisp__AzureDevOps__Organization=${AZURE_DEVOPS_ORG}
      - Crisp__AzureDevOps__Project=${AZURE_DEVOPS_PROJECT}
      - Crisp__AzureDevOps__Pat=${AZURE_DEVOPS_PAT}
      - Crisp__Common__DefaultBranch=${DEFAULT_BRANCH:-main}
      - Crisp__Common__GenerateCiCd=${GENERATE_CICD:-true}
    volumes:
      - crisp-logs:/home/crisp/logs
      - crisp-workspaces:/home/crisp/workspaces
      - crisp-sessions:/home/crisp/.crisp/sessions
    healthcheck:
      test: ["CMD", "curl", "-f", "http://localhost:5000/api/health"]
      interval: 30s
      timeout: 10s
      retries: 3
      start_period: 15s
    restart: unless-stopped
    networks:
      - cloudflared-tunnel

volumes:
  crisp-logs:
    driver: local
  crisp-workspaces:
    driver: local
  crisp-sessions:
    driver: local

networks:
  cloudflared-tunnel:
    external: true
crisp
```

> AGENT

Good. The pattern on host-node-01 is:
1. Apps go under `~/apps/` 
2. They join the `cloudflared-tunnel` external network
3. The cloudflare tunnel is token-based (managed via dashboard)

> TOOL

tool_use Write
id: toolu_01PirtX3NXtE7dubcVbUYbhj
```json
{
  "file_path": "/home/achildrenmile/bosarsalog/Dockerfile",
  "content": "# Multi-stage build\nFROM node:20-alpine AS builder\nWORKDIR /app\n\n# Install root deps\nCOPY package*.json ./\nRUN npm ci\n\n# Install server deps\nCOPY server/package*.json ./server/\nRUN cd server && npm ci\n\n# Install client deps\nCOPY client/package*.json ./client/\nRUN cd client && npm ci\n\n# Copy source and build\nCOPY tsconfig.base.json ./\nCOPY server/ ./server/\nCOPY client/ ./client/\n\n# Build client (outputs to dist/client)\nRUN cd client && npx vite build\n\n# Build server (outputs to dist/server)\nRUN cd server && npx tsc\n\nFROM node:20-alpine AS production\nRUN apk add --no-cache wget\nWORKDIR /app\nCOPY --from=builder /app/dist ./dist\nCOPY --from=builder /app/server/node_modules ./node_modules\nCOPY --from=builder /app/package.json ./\nVOLUME /data\nENV DATABASE_PATH=/data/bosarsalog.db\nENV NODE_ENV=production\nEXPOSE 3000\nCMD [\"node\", \"dist/server/index.js\"]\n"
}
```

The server TSC will output to `../dist/server` which matches the Dockerfile. But the production CMD is `node dist/server/index.js` and the output from TSC preserves the source structure. Let me check what the actual output path will be. The server `rootDir` is `src`, and the outDir is `../dist/server`. So `src/index.ts` compiles to `../dist/server/index.js`. That should work.

But the `moduleResolution: "bundler"` won't work for Node.js runtime. Let me fix that:

> TOOL

tool_use Write
id: toolu_01A6QhoVPCGffXvS1KJUtUgX
```json
{
  "file_path": "/home/achildrenmile/bosarsalog/server/tsconfig.json",
  "content": "{\n  \"extends\": \"../tsconfig.base.json\",\n  \"compilerOptions\": {\n    \"outDir\": \"../dist/server\",\n    \"rootDir\": \"src\",\n    \"module\": \"NodeNext\",\n    \"moduleResolution\": \"NodeNext\",\n    \"types\": [\"node\"]\n  },\n  \"include\": [\"src/**/*\"]\n}\n"
}
```

```json
{
  "file_path": "/home/achildrenmile/bosarsalog/deploy-production.sh",
  "content": "#!/bin/bash\nset -e\n\n# Colors for output\nRED='\\033[0;31m'\nGREEN='\\033[0;32m'\nYELLOW='\\033[1;33m'\nNC='\\033[0m' # No Color\n\n# Load environment variables\nif [ -f \".env.production\" ]; then\n    source .env.production\nelse\n    echo -e \"${RED}Error: .env.production file not found${NC}\"\n    echo \"Copy .env.production.example to .env.production and configure it\"\n    exit 1\nfi\n\n# Validate required variables\nREQUIRED_VARS=\"DEPLOY_HOST REMOTE_DIR CONTAINER_NAME IMAGE_NAME CONTAINER_PORT SITE_URL JWT_SECRET\"\nfor var in $REQUIRED_VARS; do\n    if [ -z \"${!var}\" ]; then\n        echo -e \"${RED}Error: $var is not set in .env.production${NC}\"\n        exit 1\n    fi\ndone\n\n# Check for --rebuild flag\nREBUILD_FLAG=\"\"\nif [ \"$1\" == \"--rebuild\" ]; then\n    REBUILD_FLAG=\"--no-cache\"\n    echo -e \"${YELLOW}Rebuild flag set — will build without cache${NC}\"\nfi\n\necho -e \"${GREEN}=== Deploying BOS-ARSA Log to $DEPLOY_HOST ===${NC}\"\necho \"Host: $DEPLOY_HOST\"\necho \"Remote dir: $REMOTE_DIR\"\necho \"Container: $CONTAINER_NAME\"\necho \"Port: $CONTAINER_PORT\"\necho \"\"\n\n# Step 1: Sync code to remote host\necho -e \"${GREEN}Step 1: Syncing code...${NC}\"\nGIT_URL=$(git remote get-url origin 2>/dev/null || echo \"\")\nssh \"$DEPLOY_HOST\" \"\n    if [ -d '$REMOTE_DIR' ]; then\n        cd '$REMOTE_DIR' && git pull\n    else\n        mkdir -p '$(dirname $REMOTE_DIR)'\n        git clone '$GIT_URL' '$REMOTE_DIR'\n    fi\n\"\n\n# Step 2: Copy .env.production to remote\necho -e \"${GREEN}Step 2: Copying environment config...${NC}\"\nscp .env.production \"$DEPLOY_HOST:$REMOTE_DIR/.env\"\n\n# Create .env file for docker compose\nssh \"$DEPLOY_HOST\" \"cat > '$REMOTE_DIR/.env' << 'ENVEOF'\nJWT_SECRET=$JWT_SECRET\nENVEOF\"\n\n# Step 3: Build Docker image on remote\necho -e \"${GREEN}Step 3: Building Docker image...${NC}\"\nssh \"$DEPLOY_HOST\" \"\n    cd '$REMOTE_DIR'\n    docker build $REBUILD_FLAG -t '$IMAGE_NAME' .\n\"\n\n# Step 4: Restart container via docker compose\necho -e \"${GREEN}Step 4: Restarting container...${NC}\"\nssh \"$DEPLOY_HOST\" \"\n    cd '$REMOTE_DIR'\n    docker compose down 2>/dev/null || true\n    docker compose up -d\n\"\n\n# Step 5: Run seed if first deployment (database doesn't exist yet)\necho -e \"${GREEN}Step 5: Checking database...${NC}\"\nssh \"$DEPLOY_HOST\" \"\n    # Wait for container to start\n    sleep 3\n    # Check if database has data\n    VOLUME_PATH=\\$(docker volume inspect bosarsalog_bosarsalog-data --format '{{.Mountpoint}}' 2>/dev/null || echo '')\n    if [ -n \\\"\\$VOLUME_PATH\\\" ] && [ ! -f \\\"\\$VOLUME_PATH/bosarsalog.db\\\" ]; then\n        echo 'First deployment — seeding database...'\n        docker exec $CONTAINER_NAME node -e \\\"\n            import('./dist/server/db/database.js').then(m => {\n                const db = m.initDb();\n                import('./dist/server/db/schema.js').then(s => {\n                    s.runMigrations(db);\n                    console.log('Schema created');\n                    db.close();\n                });\n            });\n        \\\" 2>/dev/null || true\n    fi\n\"\n\n# Step 6: Verify deployment\necho -e \"${GREEN}Step 6: Verifying deployment...${NC}\"\nsleep 3\n\n# Check container status\nCONTAINER_STATUS=$(ssh \"$DEPLOY_HOST\" \"docker ps --filter 'name=$CONTAINER_NAME' --format '{{.Status}}'\")\necho \"Container status: $CONTAINER_STATUS\"\n\n# Check local port\nLOCAL_PORT=$(echo $CONTAINER_PORT | cut -d: -f1)\nif ssh \"$DEPLOY_HOST\" \"curl -sf -o /dev/null http://localhost:$LOCAL_PORT/api/v1/auth/login\"; then\n    echo -e \"${GREEN}✓ Container is running on port $LOCAL_PORT${NC}\"\nelse\n    echo -e \"${YELLOW}⚠ Container not yet responding on port $LOCAL_PORT — may still be starting${NC}\"\nfi\n\n# Check public URL\nif curl -sf -o /dev/null \"$SITE_URL\" 2>/dev/null; then\n    echo -e \"${GREEN}✓ Site is accessible at $SITE_URL${NC}\"\nelse\n    echo -e \"${YELLOW}⚠ Public URL not yet accessible — configure Cloudflare tunnel for bosarsalog.oeradio.at${NC}\"\nfi\n\necho \"\"\necho -e \"${GREEN}=== Deployment complete ===${NC}\"\necho \"\"\necho \"Next steps if Cloudflare not configured:\"\necho \"  1. Go to Cloudflare Zero Trust dashboard\"\necho \"  2. Add public hostname bosarsalog.oeradio.at to the host-node-01 tunnel\"\necho \"  3. Point to http://bosarsalog:3000 (container name via cloudflared-tunnel network)\"\n"
}
```

The server startup (`index.ts`) already calls `runMigrations`, but I need a way to seed the DB. Let me create an entrypoint script that seeds on first run:

> TOOL

tool_use Write
id: toolu_01RvaDfxfycsaeUNSEwted89
```json
{
  "file_path": "/home/achildrenmile/bosarsalog/docker-entrypoint.sh",
  "content": "#!/bin/sh\nset -e\n\n# Seed database on first run if it doesn't exist or is empty\nif [ ! -f \"$DATABASE_PATH\" ] || [ ! -s \"$DATABASE_PATH\" ]; then\n    echo \"First run — seeding database...\"\n    node dist/server/db/seed.js\nfi\n\n# Start the server\nexec node dist/server/index.js\n"
}
```

```json
{
  "file_path": "/home/achildrenmile/bosarsalog/README.md",
  "content": "# BOS-ARSA Log\n\n**Krisenkommunikationsübung — Live-Erfassung, Multi-User, Auswertung**\n\nA real-time, multi-user web application for the BOS-ARSA weekly ham radio crisis communication exercises. Every Sunday, a team of admin operators queries multiple repeaters and direct frequencies across Austria to collect signal reports from ~100 amateur radio operators. BOS-ARSA Log replaces the shared Excel workbook and standalone HTML prototypes with a single unified tool.\n\nPart of the [oeradio.at](https://oeradio.at) ecosystem.\n\n## Tech Stack\n\n| Layer | Technology |\n|-------|-----------|\n| Frontend | React 18, TypeScript, Vite, Tailwind CSS, Socket.IO client |\n| Backend | Node.js, Express, TypeScript, Socket.IO server |\n| Database | SQLite via better-sqlite3 |\n| Auth | Simple callsign + PIN with JWT tokens |\n| Deployment | Docker, Docker Compose, Cloudflare Tunnel |\n\n## Architecture\n\n```\n/client          React SPA (Vite)\n/server          Express API + WebSocket server\n/data            SQLite database (volume-mounted in Docker)\n```\n\n- RESTful API for CRUD operations, WebSocket (Socket.IO) for real-time sync between concurrent admins\n- Server serves the built client in production\n- SQLite keeps deployment simple — the data volume is small (~1,000 operators, ~200 reports/week)\n\n## Key Domain Concepts\n\n| Concept | Description |\n|---------|-------------|\n| **Operator (Rufzeichen)** | Ham radio callsign, e.g. OE8YML, S55UJE |\n| **Repeater/Umsetzer** | Radio relay station with TX frequency, offset, CTCSS |\n| **Linked Repeater** | One logical repeater spanning multiple physical sites (Einstiegspunkte) |\n| **Einstiegspunkt** | Physical entry point in a linked repeater network, e.g. Hermannskogel (HK) |\n| **Signal Report (Rapport)** | RST format, e.g. \"5/9+30 HK\" = R5 / S9+30dB via Hermannskogel |\n| **Exercise (Übung)** | One Sunday session with configured repeaters and collected reports |\n| **Bezirk** | Austrian political district, identified by 2-3 letter code |\n| **Bundesland** | Austrian state (OE1=Wien through OE9=Vorarlberg) |\n\n## Features\n\n### Two Data Entry Modes\n\n- **Land (Umsetzer)** — Repeater-first entry. Select a repeater, enter reports grouped by Bezirk. Cross-repeater callsign sync: adding a callsign on one repeater auto-creates placeholder entries on all others.\n- **Bund (Bundesland)** — Geography-first entry. Bundesland/Bezirk hierarchy with inline entry per district. Einstiegspunkt auto-propagation: selecting an entry point in one Wien district auto-applies it to all Wien districts.\n\n### Live Multi-User\n\n- Socket.IO real-time sync — reports entered by one admin appear instantly for all others\n- Running totals bar always visible: participant count, total reports, per-repeater counts\n- Optimized for speed: autofocus, enter-to-submit, auto-uppercase, keyboard shortcuts\n\n### Keyboard Shortcuts\n\n| Shortcut | Action |\n|----------|--------|\n| `Alt+1` through `Alt+9` | Insert \"5/1\" through \"5/9\" in rapport field |\n| `Ctrl+1` through `Ctrl+9` | Insert \"OE1\" through \"OE9\" in callsign field |\n| `Enter` | Submit report and clear fields |\n| `Arrow Up/Down` | Navigate autocomplete suggestions |\n\n### Reports & Export\n\n- Exercise summary with per-repeater breakdown\n- Nebenstationen report (participants by district)\n- TXT export in Bund format (Bundesland-grouped) and Land format (repeater-grouped)\n\n### Operator Registry\n\n- Searchable/filterable list of all operators\n- Quick-add during live entry when callsign not found\n- Full profile with equipment, location, membership flags\n\n## Getting Started\n\n### Prerequisites\n\n- Node.js 20+\n- npm\n\n### Development\n\n```bash\n# Install dependencies\nnpm install\ncd server && npm install && cd ..\ncd client && npm install && cd ..\n\n# Seed the database with reference data\nnpm run db:seed\n\n# Start both client and server in dev mode\nnpm run dev\n```\n\nThe client runs on `http://localhost:5173` with API proxy to `http://localhost:3000`.\n\n### Default Login\n\n| Callsign | PIN | Role |\n|----------|-----|------|\n| OE8YML | changeme | admin |\n\n### Production (Docker)\n\n```bash\n# Build and run\ndocker compose up -d\n\n# Seed database (first run)\ndocker exec bosarsalog node dist/server/db/seed.js\n```\n\n### Deploy to Remote Host\n\n```bash\n# Configure deployment\ncp .env.production.example .env.production\n# Edit .env.production with your settings\n\n# Deploy\n./deploy-production.sh\n\n# Rebuild without cache\n./deploy-production.sh --rebuild\n```\n\n## API\n\nAll endpoints are prefixed with `/api/v1/`. Protected routes require `Authorization: Bearer <token>`.\n\n### Auth\n\n```\nPOST /api/v1/auth/login    { callsign, pin } -> { token, admin }\n```\n\n### Exercises\n\n```\nGET    /api/v1/exercises              List all (with summary stats)\nPOST   /api/v1/exercises              Create { date }\nGET    /api/v1/exercises/:id          Full exercise with reports\nPATCH  /api/v1/exercises/:id          Update status/notes\nGET    /api/v1/exercises/:id/stats    Live statistics\nGET    /api/v1/exercises/:id/reports  All signal reports\nPOST   /api/v1/exercises/:id/reports  Create report\nPATCH  /api/v1/exercises/:id/reports/:rid  Edit report\nDELETE /api/v1/exercises/:id/reports/:rid  Delete report\n```\n\n### Exercise Repeaters\n\n```\nGET    /api/v1/exercises/:id/repeaters\nPOST   /api/v1/exercises/:id/repeaters      Activate repeater\nPATCH  /api/v1/exercises/:id/repeaters/:rid  Update OP assignment\nDELETE /api/v1/exercises/:id/repeaters/:rid  Deactivate repeater\n```\n\n### Operators\n\n```\nGET    /api/v1/operators         List/search (?q= for autocomplete)\nPOST   /api/v1/operators         Quick-add new operator\nGET    /api/v1/operators/:id     Full profile with history\nPATCH  /api/v1/operators/:id     Update operator\n```\n\n### Reference Data\n\n```\nGET /api/v1/reference/bundeslaender\nGET /api/v1/reference/bezirke\nGET /api/v1/reference/einstiegspunkte\nGET /api/v1/repeaters\nGET /api/v1/repeaters/:id/einstiegspunkte\n```\n\n### Export\n\n```\nGET /api/v1/export/exercises/:id/bund      TXT (Bundesland-grouped)\nGET /api/v1/export/exercises/:id/land      TXT (Repeater-grouped)\nGET /api/v1/export/exercises/:id/combined  Summary report\n```\n\n### WebSocket Events (Socket.IO)\n\n```\nClient -> Server:\n  join_exercise      { exercise_id }\n  leave_exercise     { exercise_id }\n  report_created     { report data }\n  report_updated     { report data }\n  report_deleted     { report_id }\n  attendance_updated { attendance data }\n\nServer -> Client (broadcast):\n  report_created     { report, entered_by }\n  report_updated     { report, entered_by }\n  report_deleted     { report_id, entered_by }\n  attendance_updated { attendance, entered_by }\n```\n\n## Database Schema\n\n### Tables\n\n| Table | Description | Records |\n|-------|-------------|---------|\n| `bundeslaender` | Austrian states + international | 18 |\n| `bezirke` | Political districts | 84 |\n| `operators` | Ham radio operator registry | ~1,066 |\n| `repeaters` | Repeater/frequency master list | 11 |\n| `einstiegspunkte` | Entry points for linked repeaters | 12 |\n| `exercises` | Weekly exercise sessions | growing |\n| `exercise_repeaters` | Active repeaters per exercise | per exercise |\n| `exercise_attendance` | Participant tracking | per exercise |\n| `signal_reports` | The core data — signal reports | ~200/week |\n| `admins` | Admin users | ~5 |\n\n## Seed Data\n\nThe seed script (`npm run db:seed`) populates:\n\n- **9 Austrian Bundesländer** + 9 international entries (Slowenien, CZ, Deutschland, Italien, Slowakei, Kroatien, Liechtenstein, CH, Ungarn)\n- **84 Austrian Bezirke** with capital flags\n- **11 Repeaters**: Gerlitze 2m/70cm, Magdalensberg linked, Buschberg, Goldeck, Dobratsch 23cm, Struška, Hochstuhl, Zirbitzkogel, Direkte 145.300/145.525\n- **12 Einstiegspunkte** for the Magdalensberg linked system (HK, GB, NBST, JAU, FK, SCHÖ, LT, Telfs, Hochstein, Ahorn, MK, DO)\n- **Default admin** OE8YML with PIN \"changeme\"\n\n## Conventions\n\n- German UI text, English code\n- Callsigns always uppercase\n- RST format: R/S with optional +dB modifier and location suffix\n- All timestamps in Europe/Vienna timezone\n- API prefix: `/api/v1/`\n- Bezirk badges: Hauptstadt = red (#dc3545), normal = gray (#6c757d)\n- Bundesland headers with blue badges (#0d6efd)\n- Warm beige background (#faf3e4)\n\n## License\n\nMIT\n"
}
```

```
     1→import { Router } from 'express';
     2→import { getDb } from '../db/database.js';
     3→import { AuthRequest } from '../middleware/auth.js';
     4→
     5→export const exercisesRouter = Router();
     6→
     7→// List all exercises with summary stats
     8→exercisesRouter.get('/', (_req, res) => {
     9→  const db = getDb();
    10→  const exercises = db.prepare(`
    11→    SELECT e.id, e.date, e.status, e.notes,
    12→      (SELECT COUNT(DISTINCT operator_id) FROM exercise_attendance WHERE exercise_id = e.id AND is_present = 1) as participant_count,
    13→      (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM signal_reports WHERE exercise_id = e.id AND is_op_marker = 0) as report_count
    14→    FROM exercises e ORDER BY e.date DESC
    15→  `).all();
    16→  res.json(exercises);
    17→});
    18→
    19→// Create exercise
    20→exercisesRouter.post('/', (req, res) => {
    21→  const { date } = req.body;
    22→  if (!date) {
    23→    res.status(400).json({ error: 'Datum erforderlich' });
    24→    return;
    25→  }
    26→  const db = getDb();
    27→  try {
    28→    const result = db.prepare('INSERT INTO exercises (date) VALUES (?)').run(date);
    29→    const exercise = db.prepare('SELECT * FROM exercises WHERE id = ?').get(result.lastInsertRowid);
    30→    res.status(201).json(exercise);
```

> AGENT

Three problems:
1. `VOLUME /data` in Dockerfile creates an anonymous volume that can conflict with the named volume
2. The deploy script's step 5 tries to access the volume from the host (doesn't work without root)
3. The seed script destructively deletes all data

> TOOL

tool_use Edit
id: toolu_017mKcp8rRY1Lc7PVawWjG8u
```json
{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/home/achildrenmile/bosarsalog/Dockerfile",
  "old_string": "COPY docker-entrypoint.sh ./\nRUN chmod +x docker-entrypoint.sh\nVOLUME /data\nENV DATABASE_PATH=/data/bosarsalog.db",
  "new_string": "COPY docker-entrypoint.sh ./\nRUN chmod +x docker-entrypoint.sh\nRUN mkdir -p /data\nENV DATABASE_PATH=/data/bosarsalog.db"
}
```

Now the key fix — `docker compose stop` + `up -d` doesn't recreate the container or touch the volume. But we also need to handle image updates properly. Let me use the correct approach:

> TOOL

tool_use Edit
id: toolu_01Y2eCHudQy9RmEZjRRJ3Zby
```json
{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/home/achildrenmile/bosarsalog/deploy-production.sh",
  "old_string": "# Step 4: Restart container (volume is preserved — data persists)\necho -e \"${GREEN}Step 4: Restarting container...${NC}\"\nssh \"$DEPLOY_HOST\" \"\n    cd '$REMOTE_DIR'\n    docker compose stop $CONTAINER_NAME 2>/dev/null || true\n    docker compose up -d --no-recreate 2>/dev/null || docker compose up -d\n\"",
  "new_string": "# Step 4: Restart container (volume is preserved — data persists)\necho -e \"${GREEN}Step 4: Restarting container...${NC}\"\nssh \"$DEPLOY_HOST\" \"\n    cd '$REMOTE_DIR'\n    docker compose up -d --force-recreate --build\n\""
}
```

> AGENT

`docker compose up -d --force-recreate` recreates the container with the new image but **preserves named volumes**. Unlike `docker compose down` which can lose track of volumes, this is safe.

Now commit, push, and deploy — but first seed the database one last time since it was wiped:

> AGENT

Data persisted across the deploy. Fixed:

1. **Removed `VOLUME /data`** from Dockerfile — it was creating anonymous volumes that conflicted with the named volume
2. **Removed auto-seed from entrypoint** — no more accidental data wipes
3. **Replaced `docker compose down` with `up --force-recreate`** — recreates the container but preserves named volumes
4. **Seeding is now manual** — only run `docker exec bosarsalog node dist/server/db/seed.js` on first setup

```
Excellent! I've successfully retrieved all the key files. Let me now provide you with a comprehensive analysis of the BOS-ARSA Log codebase.

## Summary Report: BOS-ARSA Log Codebase Architecture

### 1. Database Schema Structure
**File:** `/home/achildrenmile/bosarsalog/server/src/db/schema.ts`

The core tables relevant to exercises and repeaters are:

- **repeaters** (lines 42-55): Stores repeater master data
  - `id`, `short_name`, `site_name`, `band`, `callsign`, `frequency_mhz`, `offset_mhz`, `ctcss_hz`, `burst_hz`
  - `type` (repeater/simplex), `is_linked` (for linked systems), `sort_order`

- **exercise_repeaters** (lines 76-81): Junction table linking exercises to repeaters
  - `exercise_id`, `repeater_id`, `operator_callsign`
  - Composite primary key on `(exercise_id, repeater_id)`
  - Stores which repeaters are active in an exercise and an optional operator callsign

- **exercises** (lines 67-74): Exercise master data
  - `id`, `name`, `date` (unique), `status` (planned/active), `notes`

- **einstiegspunkte** (lines 57-65): Entry points for linked repeaters
  - `repeater_id`, `site_name`, `callsign`, `abbreviation`, `bundesland_code`, `sort_order`
  - Represents different landing zones for linked repeater systems

- **signal_reports** (lines 92-107): Signal quality reports per operator-repeater pair
  - Unique constraint on `(exercise_id, operator_id, repeater_id)`
  - Includes readability, strength, dB over S9, einstiegspunkt reference

### 2. Seed Data
**File:** `/home/achildrenmile/bosarsalog/server/src/db/seed.ts`

Current repeaters (lines 154-172):
- **Gerlitze 2m** (OE8XNK, 145.7625 MHz)
- **Gerlitze 70cm** (OE8XNK, 439.050 MHz)
- **Magdalensberg linked** (OE8XMK, 438.575 MHz) - **LINKED SYSTEM** with 12 entry points (lines 174-198):
  - Hermannskogel (Vienna), Gaisberg (Salzburg), Nebelstein, Jauerling, Feuerkogel, Schöckl, Lachtal, Telfs, Hochstein, Ahorn, Magdalensberg, Dornbirn
- **Buschberg, Goldeck 2m, Dobratsch 23cm, Struška, Hochstuhl, Zirbitzkogel**
- **Simplex frequencies**: 145.300 MHz, 145.525 MHz

### 3. Exercise Setup Flow
**File:** `/home/achildrenmile/bosarsalog/client/src/pages/ExerciseSetupPage.tsx`

The setup page (accessed at `/exercises/:id/setup`) allows:

1. **Exercise naming** (lines 75-88):
   - Input field to set exercise name
   - Autosaves on blur via PATCH to `/api/v1/exercises/:id`

2. **Repeater activation** (lines 90-124):
   - Checkbox list of all available repeaters
   - Toggle repeaters on/off via POST/DELETE to `/api/v1/exercises/:id/repeaters/:rid`
   - For each active repeater, optional "OP Rufzeichen" (operator callsign) field
   - Updates via PATCH to `/api/v1/exercises/:id/repeaters/:rid` with `operator_callsign`

3. **Exercise activation** (lines 47-53):
   - "Übung starten" button transitions status from 'planned' to 'active'
   - Redirects to `/exercises/:id` for data entry

### 4. Exercise Routes - CRUD & Repeater Management
**File:** `/home/achildrenmile/bosarsalog/server/src/routes/exercises.ts`

**Key endpoints:**

1. **Repeater Management:**
   - `GET /:id/repeaters` (lines 118-129): List active repeaters for exercise with full details
   - `POST /:id/repeaters` (lines 131-144): Add repeater to exercise
   - `PATCH /:id/repeaters/:rid` (lines 146-152): Update operator_callsign for repeater
   - `DELETE /:id/repeaters/:rid` (lines 154-159): Remove repeater from exercise

2. **Signal Reports:**
   - `POST /:id/reports` (lines 178-217): Create signal report
     - Auto-creates placeholder entries on ALL active repeaters for new operators (lines 84-100)
     - Ensures every operator has at least one report per active repeater
   - `PATCH /:id/reports/:rid` (lines 219-244): Update report with readability, strength, dB over S9, einstiegspunkt
   - `DELETE /:id/reports/:rid` (lines 246-250): Remove report

3. **Exercise queries:**
   - `GET /:id` (lines 41-79): Full exercise with repeaters, reports, attendance
     - Reports joined with operators and repeaters
     - Sorted by bundesland_code, bezirk_code, callsign
   - `PATCH /:id` (lines 82-98): Update status, notes, name

### 5. LandMode Component - Data Entry UI
**File:** `/home/achildrenmile/bosarsalog/client/src/components/LandMode.tsx`

This is the main data entry interface displaying repeaters and signal reports:

1. **Repeater Selection** (lines 151-165):
   - Tab buttons for each active repeater
   - Shows short_name
   - Displays count of signal reports with readability data for active tab

2. **Repeater Information Display** (lines 167-176):
   - Frequency, offset, CTCSS tone, burst frequency, callsign

3. **Signal Entry Form** (lines 178-215):
   - CallsignInput component for operator selection
   - Rapport input (format: `5/9+20` → readability/strength + optional dB)
   - Notes field
   - Parse logic (lines 45-53): Regex to extract readability (first digit), strength (second digit), dB over S9 (remainder)
   - Auto-creates placeholder reports on other repeaters when operator first reports on active repeater (lines 84-100)
   - Cancel/save editing support

4. **Reports Grouped by Bundesland/Bezirk** (lines 217-284):
   - Groups non-international Bundesländer first
   - Within each Bundesland, groups by Bezirk (districts)
   - Red badge for capital cities, gray for others
   - Shows callsign, readability/strength/dB, notes
   - Click to edit, X button to delete

### Key Relationships

```
Exercise
  ├─ exercise_repeaters (many)
  │   ├─ repeater_id (FK)
  │   └─ operator_callsign (optional)
  │
  └─ signal_reports (many)
      ├─ operator_id (FK)
      ├─ repeater_id (FK)
      └─ einstiegspunkt_id (FK, for linked repeaters)

Repeater
  ├─ einstiegspunkte (many, only for linked repeaters)
```

### Critical Implementation Details

1. **Cross-repeater synchronization** (LandMode lines 84-100): When an operator submits their first report on a repeater, placeholder entries are created on all other active repeaters automatically. This ensures data consistency.

2. **Linked repeater system**: Magdalensberg is marked as `is_linked=1` with 12 associated entry points representing different landing zones across Austria.

3. **Operator callsign tracking**: Can be stored per-repeater in `exercise_repeaters.operator_callsign` (like a net control operator).

4. **Signal report uniqueness**: Enforced at DB level with unique constraint on `(exercise_id, operator_id, repeater_id)`.

All files are located at the paths provided in the environment. The system is well-structured for managing amateur radio exercises with multi-repeater support and linked repeater networks.
```

```
     1→import { Router } from 'express';
     2→import { getDb } from '../db/database.js';
     3→import { AuthRequest } from '../middleware/auth.js';
     4→
     5→export const exercisesRouter = Router();
     6→
     7→// List all exercises with summary stats
     8→exercisesRouter.get('/', (_req, res) => {
     9→  const db = getDb();
    10→  const exercises = db.prepare(`
    11→    SELECT e.id, e.date, e.status, e.notes,
    12→      (SELECT COUNT(DISTINCT operator_id) FROM exercise_attendance WHERE exercise_id = e.id AND is_present = 1) as participant_count,
    13→      (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM signal_reports WHERE exercise_id = e.id AND is_op_marker = 0) as report_count
    14→    FROM exercises e ORDER BY e.date DESC
    15→  `).all();
    16→  res.json(exercises);
    17→});
    18→
    19→// Create exercise
    20→exercisesRouter.post('/', (req, res) => {
    21→  const { date, name } = req.body;
    22→  if (!date) {
    23→    res.status(400).json({ error: 'Datum erforderlich' });
    24→    return;
    25→  }
    26→  const db = getDb();
    27→  try {
    28→    const result = db.prepare('INSERT INTO exercises (date, name) VALUES (?, ?)').run(date, name || null);
    29→    const exercise = db.prepare('SELECT * FROM exercises WHERE id = ?').get(result.lastInsertRowid);
    30→    res.status(201).json(exercise);
    31→  } catch (e: any) {
    32→    if (e.message?.includes('UNIQUE')) {
    33→      res.status(409).json({ error: 'Übung für dieses Datum existiert bereits' });
    34→    } else {
    35→      throw e;
    36→    }
    37→  }
    38→});
    39→
    40→// Get full exercise with reports
    41→exercisesRouter.get('/:id', (req, res) => {
    42→  const db = getDb();
    43→  const exercise = db.prepare('SELECT * FROM exercises WHERE id = ?').get(req.params.id);
    44→  if (!exercise) {
    45→    res.status(404).json({ error: 'Übung nicht gefunden' });
    46→    return;
    47→  }
    48→
    49→  const repeaters = db.prepare(`
    50→    SELECT er.*, r.short_name, r.site_name, r.band, r.callsign as repeater_callsign,
    51→      r.frequency_mhz, r.offset_mhz, r.ctcss_hz, r.burst_hz, r.type, r.is_linked
    52→    FROM exercise_repeaters er
    53→    JOIN repeaters r ON r.id = er.repeater_id
    54→    WHERE er.exercise_id = ?
    55→    ORDER BY r.sort_order
    56→  `).all(req.params.id);
    57→
    58→  const reports = db.prepare(`
    59→    SELECT sr.*, o.callsign, o.name as operator_name, o.bezirk_code, o.bundesland_code,
    60→      r.short_name as repeater_name,
    61→      ep.abbreviation as einstiegspunkt_abbr, ep.site_name as einstiegspunkt_name
    62→    FROM signal_reports sr
    63→    JOIN operators o ON o.id = sr.operator_id
    64→    JOIN repeaters r ON r.id = sr.repeater_id
    65→    LEFT JOIN einstiegspunkte ep ON ep.id = sr.einstiegspunkt_id
    66→    WHERE sr.exercise_id = ?
    67→    ORDER BY o.bundesland_code, o.bezirk_code, o.callsign
    68→  `).all(req.params.id);
    69→
    70→  const attendance = db.prepare(`
    71→    SELECT ea.*, o.callsign, o.name as operator_name, o.bezirk_code, o.bundesland_code
    72→    FROM exercise_attendance ea
    73→    JOIN operators o ON o.id = ea.operator_id
    74→    WHERE ea.exercise_id = ?
    75→    ORDER BY o.callsign
    76→  `).all(req.params.id);
    77→
    78→  res.json({ ...exercise, repeaters, reports, attendance });
    79→});
    80→
    81→// Update exercise status/notes
    82→exercisesRouter.patch('/:id', (req, res) => {
    83→  const db = getDb();
    84→  const { status, notes, name } = req.body;
    85→  const updates: string[] = [];
    86→  const params: any[] = [];
    87→  if (status !== undefined) { updates.push('status = ?'); params.push(status); }
    88→  if (notes !== undefined) { updates.push('notes = ?'); params.push(notes); }
    89→  if (name !== undefined) { updates.push('name = ?'); params.push(name); }
    90→  if (updates.length === 0) {
    91→    res.status(400).json({ error: 'Keine Änderungen' });
    92→    return;
    93→  }
    94→  params.push(req.params.id);
    95→  db.prepare(`UPDATE exercises SET ${updates.join(', ')} WHERE id = ?`).run(...params);
    96→  const exercise = db.prepare('SELECT * FROM exercises WHERE id = ?').get(req.params.id);
    97→  res.json(exercise);
    98→});
    99→
   100→// Get exercise stats
   101→exercisesRouter.get('/:id/stats', (req, res) => {
   102→  const db = getDb();
   103→  const eid = req.params.id;
   104→  const totalParticipants = (db.prepare('SELECT COUNT(DISTINCT operator_id) as c FROM exercise_attendance WHERE exercise_id = ? AND is_present = 1').get(eid) as any)?.c || 0;
   105→  const totalReports = (db.prepare('SELECT COUNT(*) as c FROM signal_reports WHERE exercise_id = ? AND is_op_marker = 0').get(eid) as any)?.c || 0;
   106→  const perRepeater = db.prepare(`
   107→    SELECT r.short_name, COUNT(*) as count
   108→    FROM signal_reports sr
   109→    JOIN repeaters r ON r.id = sr.repeater_id
   110→    WHERE sr.exercise_id = ? AND sr.is_op_marker = 0
   111→    GROUP BY sr.repeater_id
   112→    ORDER BY r.sort_order
   113→  `).all(eid);
   114→  res.json({ totalParticipants, totalReports, perRepeater });
   115→});
   116→

   161→// Signal Reports CRUD
   162→exercisesRouter.get('/:id/reports', (req, res) => {
   163→  const db = getDb();

   192→    `).run(req.params.id, operator_id, repeater_id, readability || null, strength || null, db_over_s9 || null, einstiegspunkt_id || null, is_op_marker ? 1 : 0, notes || null, admin.callsign);

   198→    const report = db.prepare(`

   248→  db.prepare('DELETE FROM signal_reports WHERE id = ? AND exercise_id = ?').run(req.params.rid, req.params.id);
   249→  res.json({ success: true });
   250→});
   251→
   252→// Attendance
   253→exercisesRouter.get('/:id/attendance', (req, res) => {
   254→  const db = getDb();
   255→  const attendance = db.prepare(`
   256→    SELECT ea.*, o.callsign, o.name as operator_name, o.bezirk_code, o.bundesland_code
   257→    FROM exercise_attendance ea
   258→    JOIN operators o ON o.id = ea.operator_id
   259→    WHERE ea.exercise_id = ?
   260→    ORDER BY o.callsign
   261→  `).all(req.params.id);
   262→  res.json(attendance);
   263→});
   264→
   265→exercisesRouter.post('/:id/attendance', (req, res) => {
   266→  const db = getDb();
   267→  const admin = (req as AuthRequest).admin!;
   268→  const { operator_id, callsign_suffix } = req.body;
   269→  try {
   270→    db.prepare('INSERT INTO exercise_attendance (exercise_id, operator_id, callsign_suffix, entered_by) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?)')
   271→      .run(req.params.id, operator_id, callsign_suffix || null, admin.callsign);
   272→    res.status(201).json({ success: true });
   273→  } catch (e: any) {
   274→    if (e.message?.includes('UNIQUE') || e.message?.includes('PRIMARY')) {
   275→      res.status(409).json({ error: 'Teilnehmer bereits eingetragen' });
   276→    } else {
   277→      throw e;
   278→    }
   279→  }
   280→});
   281→
   282→// Nebenstationen report
   283→exercisesRouter.get('/:id/nebenstationen', (req, res) => {
   284→  const db = getDb();
   285→  const data = db.prepare(`
   286→    SELECT bl.name as bundesland, bl.code as bundesland_code,
   287→      bz.code as bezirk_code, bz.name as bezirk_name, bz.is_capital,
   288→      COUNT(DISTINCT ea.operator_id) as count
   289→    FROM exercise_attendance ea
   290→    JOIN operators o ON o.id = ea.operator_id
   291→    JOIN bezirke bz ON bz.code = o.bezirk_code
   292→    JOIN bundeslaender bl ON bl.code = bz.bundesland_code
   293→    WHERE ea.exercise_id = ? AND ea.is_present = 1
   294→    GROUP BY bz.code
   295→    ORDER BY bl.sort_order, bz.code
   296→  `).all(req.params.id);
   297→  res.json(data);
   298→});
   299→
```

```
    14→export default function LandMode({ exerciseId, repeaters, reports, onReportCreated, onReportUpdated, onReportDeleted }: Props) {
    15→  const [activeRepeaterId, setActiveRepeaterId] = useState<number | null>(null);

    20→  // Entry form state

    22→  const [selectedOperator, setSelectedOperator] = useState<any>(null);
    23→  const [rapport, setRapport] = useState('');

    26→  const callsignRef = useRef<HTMLInputElement>(null);
    27→
    28→  useEffect(() => {
    29→    apiFetch('/api/v1/reference/bundeslaender').then(setBundeslaender).catch(() => {});
    30→    apiFetch('/api/v1/reference/bezirke').then(data => {
    31→      setAllBezirke(data);
    32→      setBezirke(data);
    33→    }).catch(() => {});
    34→  }, []);
    35→
    36→  useEffect(() => {
    37→    if (repeaters.length > 0 && !activeRepeaterId) {
    38→      setActiveRepeaterId(repeaters[0].repeater_id);
    39→    }
    40→  }, [repeaters, activeRepeaterId]);
    41→
    42→  const activeRepeater = repeaters.find(r => r.repeater_id === activeRepeaterId);
    43→  const repeaterReports = reports.filter(r => r.repeater_id === activeRepeaterId);
    44→
    45→  const parseRapport = (raw: string) => {
    46→    const match = raw.match(/^(\d)\/(\d)(.*)$/);
    47→    if (!match) return { readability: null, strength: null, db_over_s9: null };
    48→    return {
    49→      readability: parseInt(match[1]),
    50→      strength: parseInt(match[2]),
    51→      db_over_s9: match[3]?.trim() || null,
    52→    };
    53→  };
    54→
    55→  const handleSubmit = async (e: FormEvent) => {
    56→    e.preventDefault();
    57→    if (!selectedOperator || !activeRepeaterId) return;
    58→
    59→    const parsed = parseRapport(rapport);
    60→
    61→    if (editingId) {
    62→      try {
    63→        const updated = await apiFetch(`/api/v1/exercises/${exerciseId}/reports/${editingId}`, {
    64→          method: 'PATCH',
    65→          body: JSON.stringify({ ...parsed, notes: notes || null }),
    66→        });
    67→        onReportUpdated(updated);
    68→        setEditingId(null);
    69→      } catch {}
    70→    } else {
    71→      try {
    72→        const report = await apiFetch(`/api/v1/exercises/${exerciseId}/reports`, {
    73→          method: 'POST',
    74→          body: JSON.stringify({
    75→            operator_id: selectedOperator.id,
    76→            repeater_id: activeRepeaterId,
    77→            ...parsed,
    78→            notes: notes || null,
    79→          }),
    80→        });
    81→        onReportCreated(report);
    82→
    83→        // Cross-repeater sync: create placeholder entries on other repeaters
    84→        for (const rep of repeaters) {
    85→          if (rep.repeater_id !== activeRepeaterId) {
    86→            const exists = reports.some(r => r.operator_id === selectedOperator.id && r.repeater_id === rep.repeater_id);
    87→            if (!exists) {
    88→              try {
    89→                const placeholder = await apiFetch(`/api/v1/exercises/${exerciseId}/reports`, {
    90→                  method: 'POST',
    91→                  body: JSON.stringify({
    92→                    operator_id: selectedOperator.id,
    93→                    repeater_id: rep.repeater_id,
    94→                  }),
    95→                });
    96→                onReportCreated(placeholder);
    97→              } catch {}
    98→            }
    99→          }
   100→        }
   101→      } catch (err: any) {
   102→        if (err.message?.includes('existiert')) {
   103→          // Update existing
   104→          const existing = reports.find(r => r.operator_id === selectedOperator.id && r.repeater_id === activeRepeaterId);
   105→          if (existing) {
   106→            const updated = await apiFetch(`/api/v1/exercises/${exerciseId}/reports/${existing.id}`, {
   107→              method: 'PATCH',
   108→              body: JSON.stringify({ ...parsed, notes: notes || null }),
   109→            });
   110→            onReportUpdated(updated);
   111→          }
   112→        }
   113→      }
   114→    }
   115→
   116→    setCallsign('');
   117→    setSelectedOperator(null);
   118→    setRapport('');
   119→    setNotes('');
   120→  };
   121→
   122→  const handleEdit = (report: any) => {
   123→    setEditingId(report.id);
   124→    setCallsign(report.callsign);
   125→    setSelectedOperator({ id: report.operator_id, callsign: report.callsign });
   126→    const rapportStr = report.readability && report.strength
   127→      ? `${report.readability}/${report.strength}${report.db_over_s9 || ''}`
   128→      : '';
   129→    setRapport(rapportStr);
   130→    setNotes(report.notes || '');
   131→  };
   132→
   133→  const handleDelete = async (reportId: number) => {
   134→    if (!confirm('Rapport löschen?')) return;
   135→    try {
   136→      await apiFetch(`/api/v1/exercises/${exerciseId}/reports/${reportId}`, { method: 'DELETE' });
   137→      onReportDeleted(reportId);
   138→    } catch {}
   139→  };
   140→
   141→  // Group reports by Bezirk

   143→  for (const r of repeaterReports) {

   145→    if (!reportsByBezirk[key]) reportsByBezirk[key] = [];
   146→    reportsByBezirk[key].push(r);

   148→
   149→  return (
   150→    <div className="space-y-3">

   152→      <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-1">

   158→          >

   160→            {repeaterReports.length > 0 && r.repeater_id === activeRepeaterId && (
   161→              <span className="ml-1 bg-white/20 rounded-full px-1.5 text-xs">{repeaterReports.filter(rp => !rp.is_op_marker && rp.readability).length}</span>
   162→            )}
   163→          </button>
   164→        ))}
   165→      </div>
   166→
   167→      {/* Repeater info */}
   168→      {activeRepeater && (
   169→        <div className="text-xs text-gray-500 flex gap-4">
   170→          {activeRepeater.frequency_mhz && <span>{activeRepeater.frequency_mhz} MHz</span>}
   171→          {activeRepeater.offset_mhz && <span>Offset: {activeRepeater.offset_mhz > 0 ? '+' : ''}{activeRepeater.offset_mhz} MHz</span>}
   172→          {activeRepeater.ctcss_hz && <span>CTCSS: {activeRepeater.ctcss_hz} Hz</span>}
   173→          {activeRepeater.burst_hz && <span>Burst: {activeRepeater.burst_hz} Hz</span>}
   174→          {activeRepeater.repeater_callsign && <span className="font-mono">{activeRepeater.repeater_callsign}</span>}
   175→        </div>
   176→      )}
   177→
   178→      {/* Entry form */}
   179→      <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="bg-white rounded-lg shadow-sm p-3 flex items-center gap-2 flex-wrap">
   180→        <CallsignInput
   181→          value={callsign}
   182→          onChange={setCallsign}
   183→          onSelect={setSelectedOperator}
   184→          autoFocus
   185→          className="w-36"
   186→        />
   187→        <input

   189→          value={rapport}
   190→          onChange={e => setRapport(e.target.value.toUpperCase())}
   191→          placeholder="5/9+20"
   192→          className="border border-gray-300 rounded px-2 py-1 font-mono text-sm w-24 focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-amber-500"
   193→          onKeyDown={e => {

   196→              setRapport(`5/${e.key}`);
   197→            }
   198→          }}
   199→        />
   200→        <input
   201→          type="text"
   202→          value={notes}
   203→          onChange={e => setNotes(e.target.value)}
   204→          placeholder="Sonstiges"

   206→        />
   207→        <button type="submit" className="bg-[#5b3a1a] hover:bg-[#7a5230] text-white px-4 py-1 rounded text-sm font-medium">

   217→      {/* Reports grouped by Bezirk */}
```
