     1→import { useState, useEffect, useCallback, useRef } from 'react';
     2→import { useParams, Link } from 'react-router-dom';
     3→import { apiFetch } from '../services/api';
     4→import { getSocket } from '../services/socket';
     5→import { useAuth } from '../hooks/useAuth';
     6→import LandMode from '../components/LandMode';
     7→import BundMode from '../components/BundMode';
     8→import RunningTotals from '../components/RunningTotals';
     9→
    10→interface Stats {
    11→  totalParticipants: number;
    12→  totalReports: number;
    13→  perRepeater: { short_name: string; count: number }[];
    14→}
    15→
    16→export default function ExercisePage() {
    17→  const { id } = useParams<{ id: string }>();
    18→  const { admin } = useAuth();
    19→  const [exercise, setExercise] = useState<any>(null);
    20→  const [mode, setMode] = useState<'land' | 'bund'>('land');
    21→  const [stats, setStats] = useState<Stats>({ totalParticipants: 0, totalReports: 0, perRepeater: [] });
    22→  const [reports, setReports] = useState<any[]>([]);
    23→  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
    24→  const socketRef = useRef(getSocket());
    25→
    26→  const refreshStats = useCallback(async () => {
    27→    if (!id) return;
    28→    try {
    29→      const s = await apiFetch(`/api/v1/exercises/${id}/stats`);
    30→      setStats(s);
    31→    } catch {}
    32→  }, [id]);
    33→
    34→  useEffect(() => {
    35→    if (!id) return;
    36→    setLoading(true);
    37→    apiFetch(`/api/v1/exercises/${id}`)
    38→      .then(data => {
    39→        setExercise(data);
    40→        setReports(data.reports || []);
    41→      })
    42→      .catch(() => {})
    43→      .finally(() => setLoading(false));
    44→    refreshStats();
    45→  }, [id, refreshStats]);
    46→
    47→  useEffect(() => {
    48→    if (!id) return;
    49→    const socket = socketRef.current;
    50→    socket.emit('join_exercise', { exercise_id: parseInt(id) });
    51→
    52→    socket.on('report_created', (data: any) => {
    53→      setReports(prev => [data, ...prev]);
    54→      refreshStats();
    55→    });
    56→    socket.on('report_updated', (data: any) => {
    57→      setReports(prev => prev.map(r => r.id === data.id ? data : r));
    58→      refreshStats();
    59→    });
    60→    socket.on('report_deleted', (data: any) => {
    61→      setReports(prev => prev.filter(r => r.id !== data.report_id));
    62→      refreshStats();
    63→    });
    64→
    65→    return () => {
    66→      socket.emit('leave_exercise', { exercise_id: parseInt(id) });
    67→      socket.off('report_created');
    68→      socket.off('report_updated');
    69→      socket.off('report_deleted');
    70→    };
    71→  }, [id, refreshStats]);
    72→
    73→  const handleReportCreated = useCallback((report: any) => {
    74→    setReports(prev => [report, ...prev]);
    75→    socketRef.current.emit('report_created', { ...report, exercise_id: parseInt(id!) });
    76→    refreshStats();
    77→  }, [id, refreshStats]);
    78→
    79→  const handleReportUpdated = useCallback((report: any) => {
    80→    setReports(prev => prev.map(r => r.id === report.id ? report : r));
    81→    socketRef.current.emit('report_updated', { ...report, exercise_id: parseInt(id!) });
    82→    refreshStats();
    83→  }, [id, refreshStats]);
    84→
    85→  const handleReportDeleted = useCallback((reportId: number) => {
    86→    setReports(prev => prev.filter(r => r.id !== reportId));
    87→    socketRef.current.emit('report_deleted', { report_id: reportId, exercise_id: parseInt(id!) });
    88→    refreshStats();
    89→  }, [id, refreshStats]);
    90→
    91→  const handleStatusChange = async (newStatus: string) => {
    92→    if (!id) return;
    93→    await apiFetch(`/api/v1/exercises/${id}`, {
    94→      method: 'PATCH',
    95→      body: JSON.stringify({ status: newStatus }),
    96→    });
    97→    setExercise((prev: any) => prev ? { ...prev, status: newStatus } : null);
    98→  };
    99→
   100→  if (loading) return <p className="text-gray-500 p-4">Laden...</p>;
   101→  if (!exercise) return <p className="text-red-500 p-4">Übung nicht gefunden</p>;
   102→
   103→  const statusColor = exercise.status === 'active' ? 'bg-green-500' : exercise.status === 'completed' ? 'bg-blue-500' : 'bg-gray-400';
   104→  const statusLabel = exercise.status === 'active' ? 'Aktiv' : exercise.status === 'completed' ? 'Abgeschlossen' : 'Geplant';
   105→
   106→  return (
   107→    <div className="space-y-4">
   108→      <div className="flex items-center justify-between flex-wrap gap-2">
   109→        <div className="flex items-center gap-4">
   110→          <h1 className="text-xl font-bold text-[#5b3a1a]">
   111→            {exercise.name || 'Übung'} — {new Date(exercise.date + 'T00:00:00').toLocaleDateString('de-AT', { day: '2-digit', month: '2-digit', year: 'numeric' })}
   112→          </h1>
   113→          <span className={`px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full text-xs text-white ${statusColor}`}>{statusLabel}</span>
   114→        </div>
   115→        <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
   116→          {exercise.status === 'planned' && (
   117→            <button onClick={() => handleStatusChange('active')} className="bg-green-600 hover:bg-green-700 text-white px-3 py-1 rounded text-sm">
   118→              Starten
   119→            </button>
   120→          )}
   121→          {exercise.status === 'active' && (
   122→            <button onClick={() => handleStatusChange('completed')} className="bg-blue-600 hover:bg-blue-700 text-white px-3 py-1 rounded text-sm">
   123→              Abschließen
   124→            </button>
   125→          )}
   126→          <Link to={`/exercises/${id}/setup`} className="bg-gray-200 hover:bg-gray-300 text-gray-700 px-3 py-1 rounded text-sm">
   127→            Einrichten
   128→          </Link>
   129→          <Link to={`/exercises/${id}/reports`} className="bg-amber-100 hover:bg-amber-200 text-amber-800 px-3 py-1 rounded text-sm">
   130→            Auswertung
   131→          </Link>
   132→        </div>
   133→      </div>
   134→
   135→      <RunningTotals stats={stats} />
   136→
   137→      <div className="flex gap-1 bg-white rounded-lg p-1 shadow-sm w-fit">
   138→        <button
   139→          onClick={() => setMode('land')}
   140→          className={`px-4 py-1.5 rounded text-sm font-medium ${mode === 'land' ? 'bg-[#5b3a1a] text-white' : 'text-gray-600 hover:bg-gray-100'}`}
   141→        >
   142→          Land (Umsetzer)
   143→        </button>
   144→        <button
   145→          onClick={() => setMode('bund')}
   146→          className={`px-4 py-1.5 rounded text-sm font-medium ${mode === 'bund' ? 'bg-[#5b3a1a] text-white' : 'text-gray-600 hover:bg-gray-100'}`}
   147→        >
   148→          Bund (Bundesland)
   149→        </button>
   150→      </div>
   151→
   152→      {mode === 'land' ? (
   153→        <LandMode
   154→          exerciseId={parseInt(id!)}
   155→          repeaters={exercise.repeaters || []}
   156→          reports={reports}
   157→          onReportCreated={handleReportCreated}
   158→          onReportUpdated={handleReportUpdated}
   159→          onReportDeleted={handleReportDeleted}
   160→        />
   161→      ) : (

   163→          exerciseId={parseInt(id!)}

   165→          reports={reports}
   166→          onReportCreated={handleReportCreated}
   167→          onReportUpdated={handleReportUpdated}
   168→          onReportDeleted={handleReportDeleted}
   169→        />
   170→      )}
   171→    </div>
   172→  );
   173→}

     5→interface Props {

    22→export default function BundMode({ exerciseId, repeaters, reports, onReportCreated, onReportUpdated, onReportDeleted }: Props) {
    23→  const [bundeslaender, setBundeslaender] = useState<any[]>([]);
    24→  const [bezirke, setBezirke] = useState<any[]>([]);
    25→  const [einstiegspunkte, setEinstiegspunkte] = useState<any[]>([]);
    26→  const [collapsedBl, setCollapsedBl] = useState<Set<string>>(new Set());
    27→  const [blEpSelection, setBlEpSelection] = useState<Record<string, number | null>>({});
    28→  const [editingId, setEditingId] = useState<number | null>(null);
    29→  const [editForm, setEditForm] = useState<EntryForm>({ callsign: '', operator: null, rapport: '', einstiegspunktId: null, notes: '' });
    30→
    31→  // Linked repeater for Einstiegspunkt dropdown
    32→  const linkedRepeater = repeaters.find(r => r.is_linked);
    33→
    34→  useEffect(() => {
    35→    apiFetch('/api/v1/reference/bundeslaender').then(setBundeslaender).catch(() => {});
    36→    apiFetch('/api/v1/reference/bezirke').then(setBezirke).catch(() => {});
    37→  }, []);
    38→
    39→  useEffect(() => {
    40→    if (linkedRepeater) {
    41→      apiFetch(`/api/v1/repeaters/${linkedRepeater.repeater_id}/einstiegspunkte`)
    42→        .then(setEinstiegspunkte).catch(() => {});
    43→    }
    44→  }, [linkedRepeater]);
    45→
    46→  const toggleBl = (code: string) => {
    47→    setCollapsedBl(prev => {
    48→      const next = new Set(prev);
    49→      if (next.has(code)) next.delete(code); else next.add(code);
    50→      return next;
    51→    });
    52→  };
    53→
    54→  const parseRapport = (raw: string) => {
    55→    const match = raw.match(/^(\d)\/(\d)(.*)$/);
    56→    if (!match) return { readability: null, strength: null, db_over_s9: null };
    57→    return {
    58→      readability: parseInt(match[1]),
    59→      strength: parseInt(match[2]),
    60→      db_over_s9: match[3]?.trim() || null,
    61→    };
    62→  };
    63→
    64→  const handleBezirkSubmit = async (bezirkCode: string, bundeslandCode: string, form: EntryForm) => {
    65→    if (!form.operator) return;
    66→    if (!linkedRepeater) return;
    67→
    68→    const parsed = parseRapport(form.rapport);
    69→    const epId = form.einstiegspunktId || blEpSelection[bundeslandCode] || null;
    70→
    71→    try {
    72→      const report = await apiFetch(`/api/v1/exercises/${exerciseId}/reports`, {
    73→        method: 'POST',
    74→        body: JSON.stringify({
    75→          operator_id: form.operator.id,
    76→          repeater_id: linkedRepeater.repeater_id,
    77→          ...parsed,
    78→          einstiegspunkt_id: epId,
    79→          notes: form.notes || null,
    80→        }),
    81→      });
    82→      onReportCreated(report);
    83→    } catch (err: any) {
    84→      if (err.message?.includes('existiert')) {
    85→        const existing = reports.find(r => r.operator_id === form.operator.id && r.repeater_id === linkedRepeater.repeater_id);
    86→        if (existing) {
    87→          const updated = await apiFetch(`/api/v1/exercises/${exerciseId}/reports/${existing.id}`, {
    88→            method: 'PATCH',
    89→            body: JSON.stringify({ ...parsed, einstiegspunkt_id: epId, notes: form.notes || null }),
    90→          });
    91→          onReportUpdated(updated);
    92→        }
    93→      }
    94→    }
    95→  };
    96→
    97→  const handleEpChange = (bundeslandCode: string, epId: number | null) => {
    98→    // Auto-propagate: set this EP for the entire Bundesland
    99→    setBlEpSelection(prev => ({ ...prev, [bundeslandCode]: epId }));
   100→  };
   101→
   102→  const handleEdit = (report: any) => {
   103→    setEditingId(report.id);
   104→    setEditForm({
   105→      callsign: report.callsign,
   106→      operator: { id: report.operator_id, callsign: report.callsign },
   107→      rapport: report.readability && report.strength ? `${report.readability}/${report.strength}${report.db_over_s9 || ''}` : '',
   108→      einstiegspunktId: report.einstiegspunkt_id || null,
   109→      notes: report.notes || '',
   110→    });
   111→  };
   112→
   113→  const handleUpdate = async () => {
   114→    if (!editingId) return;
   115→    const parsed = parseRapport(editForm.rapport);
   116→    try {
   117→      const updated = await apiFetch(`/api/v1/exercises/${exerciseId}/reports/${editingId}`, {
   118→        method: 'PATCH',
   119→        body: JSON.stringify({ ...parsed, einstiegspunkt_id: editForm.einstiegspunktId, notes: editForm.notes || null }),
   120→      });
   121→      onReportUpdated(updated);
   122→      setEditingId(null);
   123→    } catch {}
   124→  };
   125→
   126→  const handleDelete = async (reportId: number) => {
   127→    if (!confirm('Rapport löschen?')) return;
   128→    try {
   129→      await apiFetch(`/api/v1/exercises/${exerciseId}/reports/${reportId}`, { method: 'DELETE' });
   130→      onReportDeleted(reportId);
   131→    } catch {}
   132→  };
   133→
   134→  return (
   135→    <div className="space-y-2">
   136→      {bundeslaender.filter(bl => !bl.is_international).map(bl => {
   137→        const blBezirke = bezirke.filter(b => b.bundesland_code === bl.code);
   138→        const blReports = reports.filter(r => r.bundesland_code === bl.code && linkedRepeater && r.repeater_id === linkedRepeater.repeater_id);
   139→        const isCollapsed = collapsedBl.has(bl.code);
   140→        const reportCount = blReports.filter(r => !r.is_op_marker && r.readability).length;
   141→
   142→        return (
   143→          <div key={bl.code} className="bg-white rounded-lg shadow-sm overflow-hidden">
   144→            {/* Bundesland header */}
   145→            <div
   146→              onClick={() => toggleBl(bl.code)}
   147→              className="bg-gray-50 px-3 py-2 flex items-center justify-between cursor-pointer hover:bg-gray-100 border-b"
   148→            >
   149→              <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
   150→                <span className="text-xs text-gray-400">{isCollapsed ? '▶' : '▼'}</span>
   151→                <span className="font-medium text-sm">{bl.name}</span>
   152→                <span className="bg-[#0d6efd] text-white text-xs px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded-full">{reportCount}</span>
   153→              </div>
   154→              {linkedRepeater && einstiegspunkte.length > 0 && (
   155→                <select
   156→                  value={blEpSelection[bl.code] || ''}
   157→                  onChange={e => {
   158→                    e.stopPropagation();
   159→                    handleEpChange(bl.code, e.target.value ? parseInt(e.target.value) : null);
   160→                  }}
   161→                  onClick={e => e.stopPropagation()}
   162→                  className="text-xs border border-gray-300 rounded px-1.5 py-0.5"
   163→                >
   164→                  <option value="">Einstiegspunkt</option>
   165→                  {einstiegspunkte.map(ep => (
   166→                    <option key={ep.id} value={ep.id}>{ep.abbreviation} ({ep.site_name})</option>
   167→                  ))}
   168→                </select>
   169→              )}
   170→            </div>
   171→
   172→            {/* Bezirk rows */}
   173→            {!isCollapsed && (
   174→              <div className="divide-y divide-gray-100">
   175→                {blBezirke.map(bz => {
   176→                  const bzReports = blReports.filter(r => r.bezirk_code === bz.code);
   177→                  return (
   178→                    <BezirkRow
   179→                      key={bz.code}
   180→                      bezirk={bz}
   181→                      reports={bzReports}
   182→                      einstiegspunkte={einstiegspunkte}
   183→                      defaultEpId={blEpSelection[bl.code] || null}
   184→                      onSubmit={(form) => handleBezirkSubmit(bz.code, bl.code, form)}
   185→                      onEdit={handleEdit}
   186→                      onDelete={handleDelete}
   187→                      editingId={editingId}
   188→                      editForm={editForm}
   189→                      onEditChange={setEditForm}
   190→                      onEditSave={handleUpdate}
   191→                      onEditCancel={() => setEditingId(null)}
   192→                    />
   193→                  );
   194→                })}
   195→              </div>
   196→            )}
   197→          </div>
   198→        );
   199→      })}
   200→    </div>
   201→  );
   202→}
   203→
   204→interface BezirkRowProps {
   205→  bezirk: any;
   206→  reports: any[];
   207→  einstiegspunkte: any[];
   208→  defaultEpId: number | null;
   209→  onSubmit: (form: EntryForm) => void;

   218→
   219→function BezirkRow({ bezirk, reports, einstiegspunkte, defaultEpId, onSubmit, onEdit, onDelete, editingId, editForm, onEditChange, onEditSave, onEditCancel }: BezirkRowProps) {
   220→  const [form, setForm] = useState<EntryForm>({ callsign: '', operator: null, rapport: '', einstiegspunktId: null, notes: '' });
   221→
   222→  const handleSubmit = (e: FormEvent) => {
   223→    e.preventDefault();
   224→    if (!form.operator) return;
   225→    onSubmit({ ...form, einstiegspunktId: form.einstiegspunktId || defaultEpId });
   226→    setForm({ callsign: '', operator: null, rapport: '', einstiegspunktId: null, notes: '' });
   227→  };
   228→
   229→  return (
   230→    <div className="px-3 py-1.5">
   231→      <div className="flex items-center gap-2 mb-1">
   232→        <span className={`text-xs font-mono px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded text-white ${bezirk.is_capital ? 'bg-[#dc3545]' : 'bg-[#6c757d]'}`}>
   233→          {bezirk.code}
   234→        </span>
   235→        <span className="text-xs text-gray-500">{bezirk.name}</span>
   236→      </div>
   237→
   238→      {/* Existing reports */}
   239→      <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-1 mb-1">
   240→        {reports.map(r => (
   241→          <div key={r.id} className="group">
   242→            {editingId === r.id ? (
   243→              <div className="flex items-center gap-1 bg-amber-50 rounded p-1">
   244→                <input value={editForm.rapport} onChange={e => onEditChange({ ...editForm, rapport: e.target.value.toUpperCase() })} className="border rounded px-1 py-0.5 font-mono text-xs w-20" />
   245→                <button onClick={onEditSave} className="text-green-600 text-xs font-bold">OK</button>
   246→                <button onClick={onEditCancel} className="text-gray-400 text-xs">X</button>
   247→              </div>
   248→            ) : (
   249→              <span
   250→                onClick={() => onEdit(r)}
   251→                className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 bg-gray-50 hover:bg-amber-50 rounded px-1.5 py-0.5 cursor-pointer text-xs"
   252→              >
   253→                <span className="font-mono font-medium">{r.callsign}</span>
   254→                {r.readability && r.strength && (
   255→                  <span className="text-gray-500">{r.readability}/{r.strength}{r.db_over_s9 || ''}</span>
   256→                )}
   257→                {r.einstiegspunkt_abbr && <span className="text-blue-600">{r.einstiegspunkt_abbr}</span>}
   258→                {r.notes && <span className="text-amber-600">({r.notes})</span>}
   259→                <button
   260→                  onClick={e => { e.stopPropagation(); onDelete(r.id); }}
   261→                  className="text-red-400 hover:text-red-600 opacity-0 group-hover:opacity-100"
   262→                >
   263→                  x
   264→                </button>
   265→              </span>
   266→            )}
   267→          </div>
   268→        ))}
   269→      </div>
   270→
   271→      {/* Inline entry form */}
   272→      <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="flex items-center gap-1">
   273→        <CallsignInput
   274→          value={form.callsign}
   275→          onChange={v => setForm(f => ({ ...f, callsign: v }))}
   276→          onSelect={op => setForm(f => ({ ...f, operator: op, callsign: op.callsign }))}
   277→          className="w-28"
   278→        />
   279→        <input
   280→          type="text"
   281→          value={form.rapport}
   282→          onChange={e => setForm(f => ({ ...f, rapport: e.target.value.toUpperCase() }))}
   283→          placeholder="5/9"
   284→          className="border border-gray-300 rounded px-1.5 py-0.5 font-mono text-xs w-20 focus:outline-none focus:ring-1 focus:ring-amber-500"
   285→          onKeyDown={e => {
   286→            if (e.altKey && e.key >= '1' && e.key <= '9') {
   287→              e.preventDefault();
   288→              setForm(f => ({ ...f, rapport: `5/${e.key}` }));
   289→            }
   290→          }}
   291→        />
   292→        {einstiegspunkte.length > 0 && (
   293→          <select
   294→            value={form.einstiegspunktId || defaultEpId || ''}
   295→            onChange={e => setForm(f => ({ ...f, einstiegspunktId: e.target.value ? parseInt(e.target.value) : null }))}
   296→            className="text-xs border border-gray-300 rounded px-1 py-0.5"
   297→          >
   298→            <option value="">EP</option>
   299→            {einstiegspunkte.map(ep => (
   300→              <option key={ep.id} value={ep.id}>{ep.abbreviation}</option>
   301→            ))}
   302→          </select>
   303→        )}
   304→        <input

   306→          value={form.notes}
   307→          onChange={e => setForm(f => ({ ...f, notes: e.target.value }))}

   310→        />
   311→        <button type="submit" className="bg-[#5b3a1a] hover:bg-[#7a5230] text-white px-2 py-0.5 rounded text-xs">
   312→          +
   313→        </button>
   314→      </form>
   315→    </div>
   316→  );

     4→export const referenceRouter = Router();

     9→});

    16→  } else {
    17→    res.json(db.prepare('SELECT * FROM bezirke ORDER BY bundesland_code, code').all());
    18→  }
    19→});
    20→
    21→referenceRouter.get('/einstiegspunkte', (req, res) => {
    22→  const db = getDb();

    24→  if (rid) {

    26→  } else {
    27→    res.json(db.prepare('SELECT * FROM einstiegspunkte ORDER BY repeater_id, sort_order').all());
    28→  }
    29→});

  "content": "# Landesweite Restructuring — Implementation Plan\n\n## Context\nThe user wants exercises to support \"Landesweite\" (state-wide) queries organized by Bundesland (OE1-OE9). The exercise setup needs restructuring so admins can select participating Bundesländer, see all repeaters per Bundesland, assign operators per repeater, and add custom repeaters/frequencies during setup.
